### [1] [Aspects of Anomalies: Fermion Family Constraints, Gravitational Form Factors and Scattering Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26893)
*Dario Melle*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨量子反常在量子场论中的双重作用：作为约束理论场内容的一致性条件，以及作为相关函数解析结构中编码的动力学效应。第一部分研究331模型及其扩展，第二部分利用动量空间共形场论研究手征、引力和共形反常，并将其应用于强子物理和散射过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子反常既是量子场论自洽性的关键约束（如异常相消），也蕴含丰富的动力学信息（如反常极点、谱密度关系及对强子结构的影响）。本文旨在系统揭示其在粒子物理模型构建与高能现象学中的双重角色。

Method: 采用理论分析与计算方法：包括规范反常抵消分析、对称性破缺与标量/味物理研究、TeV尺度Landau极点估算、高能对撞机唯象学模拟；并运用动量空间共形场论、非阿贝尔TJJ等关联函数的离壳参数化、谱密度反常求和规则证明、Sudakov重求和及双拷贝类结构分析。

Result: 1）331模型结合QCD渐近自由可自然导出三代费米子；其新型末态（同号双μ子＋b喷注）可在100 TeV对撞机观测；左-右对称33311扩展避免质子衰变并保持代数约束。2）构造了QCD中非阿贝尔TJJ关联函数的完整离壳参数化，识别出类稀松子反常极点；证明AVV/ATT/TJJ顶点的质量无关反常求和规则；反常极点非物理渐近态；TJJ插入显著改善π介子引力形状因子（尤其D项与迹项）与格点QCD的符合度；双反常顶点通过引力子传播给出规范不变的标量引力通道，呈现光子-引力子散射的双拷贝结构。

Conclusion: 量子反常不仅是理论自洽的‘守门人’，更是连接紫外结构、红外现象与数值模拟的重要桥梁；本工作统一处理模型构建与反常动力学，为新物理探索与强子结构理解提供了新视角与工具。

Abstract: Quantum anomalies play two complementary roles in quantum field theory: as consistency conditions that constrain the field content of a theory, and as dynamical effects encoded in the analytic structure of correlation functions. This thesis explores both. In the first part, we study the 331 model, in which gauge anomalies cancel across families rather than within each one. Combined with QCD asymptotic freedom, this fixes the number of fermion generations to three. We analyze the model's symmetry breaking, scalar and flavor sectors, and its TeV-scale Landau pole. We then study the collider phenomenology of its exotic quarks, showing that the pair production of heavy quarks decaying into doubly-charged vector bileptons gives a clean same-sign dimuon plus b-jets signature, observable at a 100 TeV collider but not at the LHC. Finally, we introduce the left-right symmetric 33311 extension, which preserves the family-number constraint and avoids proton decay. In the second part, we use conformal field theory in momentum space to study the chiral, gravitational and conformal anomalies. We construct a complete off-shell parametrization of the non-Abelian TJJ correlator in perturbative QCD, identifying the dilaton-like anomaly pole. For the AVV, ATT and TJJ vertices, we prove mass-independent anomaly sum rules for the spectral density, showing that the anomaly pole is not an asymptotic particle state. We apply the TJJ insertion to the pion gravitational form factors at large momentum transfer with Sudakov resummation: the anomaly feeds mainly into the D-term and the trace form factor, improving agreement with lattice QCD. Lastly, sewing two anomaly vertices through graviton exchange, we show that the trace anomaly activates a gauge-invariant scalar gravitational channel, giving a double-copy-like structure in photon and graviton scattering.

</details>


### [2] [Floquet Instability of Photon-Axion Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26908)
*Yuuki Sugiyama,Kazushige Ueda*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有时空周期性的背景磁场中轴子-光子转换现象，利用Floquet定理分析发现存在由Floquet不稳定性引起的指数增长模式，并表明该不稳定性可增强由初始光子场产生的轴子场振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找轴子是探索超越标准模型物理的重要途径，而轴子-光子转换是其关键探测机制之一；本文旨在探究时空周期性磁场对这一转换过程的新影响。

Method: 通过洛伦兹变换将轴子-光子耦合系统映射为时间周期性线性系统，应用Floquet定理分析其不稳定性，并进行微扰分析确定不稳定参数区域。

Result: 发现了与Floquet副本共振交叉相关的不稳定模式，表现为复Floquet指数导致的指数增长；确定了该Floquet不稳定性出现的参数区域；证实该不稳定性可增强轴子场振幅。

Conclusion: 时空周期性磁场可通过Floquet不稳定性显著改变轴子-光子转换行为，提供了一种新的轴子产生与探测机制。

Abstract: The discovery of axions would provide evidence for physics beyond the Standard Model. In this work, we investigate axion-photon conversion in the presence of a background magnetic field with simultaneous spatial and temporal periodicity. By performing a Lorentz transformation, the coupled axion-photon system is mapped onto a time-periodic linear system that can be analyzed using Floquet theorem. We find unstable modes associated with resonant crossing between Floquet replicas. The resulting complex Floquet exponents lead to exponentially growing modes, which we identify as a Floquet instability. Then, we determine the parameter regions in which this instability appears in a perturbative analysis. We further show that the Floquet instability enhances the axion-field amplitude generated from an initial photon field. Our results demonstrate that spatiotemporally periodic magnetic backgrounds provide a distinct mechanism for modifying axion-photon conversion through Floquet instability.

</details>

*Ijaz Ahmed,Waqas Ahmad,M. Tayyab Javaid,Jamil Muhammad*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在3 TeV和10 TeV缪子对撞机上，通过单光子信号μ⁺μ⁻→γa→γνν̄探测类轴子粒子（ALP）的灵敏度，采用双耦合ALP简化模型，并结合传统切口分析与基于TMVA的梯度提升决策树方法进行信号识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高能缪子对撞机在探测极弱耦合、不可见衰变主导的ALP新物理方面的独特潜力，弥补LHC等强子对撞机在该参数空间的探测盲区。

Method: 在两耦合ALP简化模型下，模拟五个质量基准点（100–1000 GeV），固定光子耦合gaγγ=0.01 TeV⁻¹及中微子赝标耦合yν=0.1或0.3；采用切口分析与ROOT/TMVA梯度提升决策树（GBDT）两种方法优化单光子信号选择，并评估背景系统误差影响。

Result: GBDT方法在10 TeV下达到最优灵敏度g₉₅≈0.0122–0.0144 TeV⁻¹，显著优于切口法；因不可约背景事例极少（仅2–41个），结果以预期灵敏度等高线形式给出，而非CLs排除限；附录中还开展了HNL双光子唯象可行性研究。

Conclusion: 缪子对撞机上的单光子道是探测高质ALP与不可见衰变的理想探针，机器学习方法可大幅提升探测灵敏度，为未来缪子对撞机物理规划提供关键理论支持。

Abstract: We study the mono-photon signature $μ^+μ^-\toγa\toγν\barν$ at $3$ and $10\TeV$ muon colliders, with integrated luminosities of $1$ and $10\,\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, in a two-coupling axion-like-particle (ALP) simplified model. Five benchmarks spanning $m_a=100$--$1000\GeV$ are generated at $g_{aγγ}=0.01\TeV^{-1}$ and flavor-universal pseudoscalar neutrino couplings $y_ν=0.1$ or $0.3$, giving invisible branching fractions above 99.995\% and mono-photon cross sections of $0.00854$--$0.01217\,\mathrm{fb}$. Assuming a 1\% background normalization uncertainty, a cut-based photon-line selection gives projected 95\% sensitivity to $g_{aγγ}\simeq0.039$--$0.045\TeV^{-1}$ at $3\TeV$ and $0.0137$--$0.0140\TeV^{-1}$ at $10\TeV$. A ROOT/TMVA gradient-boosted decision tree with validation-fixed thresholds reaches held-out AUC values of $0.9967$--$0.9993$ and $g_{95}=0.0192$--$0.0400\TeV^{-1}$ at $3\TeV$ and $0.0122$--$0.0144\TeV^{-1}$ at $10\TeV$. Because only 2-41 irreducible-background test events populate the optimized classifier tails, the mass--coupling contours are reported as projected expected sensitivities rather than profile-likelihood or CL$_s$ exclusions. A separate appendix records a phenomenological, parton-level HNL diphoton feasibility study; it is not used to claim detector-level sensitivity or exclusion.} \keywords{Axion-like particles, muon collider, mono-photon, diphoton, heavy neutral leptons, neutrinos, multivariate analysis, effective field theory

</details>

*Diganta Das,Vedant Parekh,Ria Sain,Dargi Shameer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了Λ_b → Λℓ⁺ℓ⁻和Λ_b → Λ*ℓ⁺ℓ⁻稀有衰变中的轻子极化可观测量，基于有效哈密顿量框架，利用螺旋度方法导出单/双轻子极化不对称性和极化前后不对称性，并结合最新格点QCD形状因子进行数值计算，研究其对q²依赖性及对超出标准模型新物理的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Λ_b到Λ和Λ*的稀有衰变由b→sℓ⁺ℓ⁻过程主导，对标准模型内及之外的短距物理高度敏感，亟需系统分析轻子极化可观测量以探测新物理信号。

Method: 采用有效哈密顿量框架，运用螺旋度方法推导单、双轻子极化不对称性及极化前后不对称性；所有可观测量均用横动量振幅表示，并输入当前最精确的格点QCD计算得到的强子形状因子。

Result: 给出了Λ（基态）与Λ*(1520)（激发态）末态下多个极化可观测量随q²变化的完整行为；明确了各可观测量对有效算符手征结构及复Wilson系数的差异化敏感性。

Conclusion: 轻子极化可观测量是区分标准模型与新物理手征结构、约束复Wilson系数的理想探针，尤其在Λ*末态中展现出独特灵敏度，为LHCb等实验提供关键理论指导。

Abstract: We present an analysis of lepton polarization observables in the rare flavor-changing neutral current decays $Λ_b \to Λ\ell^+\ell^-$ and $Λ_b \to Λ^*\ell^+\ell^-$. These decays are driven by the quark-level transition $b \to s\ell^+\ell^-$ and are sensitive to short-distance physics within and beyond the Standard Model. We work within an effective Hamiltonian framework and derive single lepton polarization asymmetries, double lepton polarization asymmetries, and polarized forward-backward asymmetries using the helicity method. The observables are expressed in terms of transversity amplitudes and evaluated using state-of-the-art lattice QCD form factors. We analyze the $q^2$ dependence of several observables for both the ground-state $Λ$ and the excited $Λ^*(1520)$ final states and discuss sensitivities of the observables to chiral structure of the effective operators and complex Wilson coefficients.

</details>


### [5] [Flavor decomposition of electromagnetic multipole moments in $[q_1q_2][\bar q_3 \bar c]$ tetraquarks: Light-quark analogs of $T_{cs1}(2900)^0$ with $J^P = 1^+$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27843)
*Ulaş Özdem*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了具有$J^P = 1^+$量子数的紧凑型$[q_1 q_2][\bar q_3 \bar c]$四夸克态的磁偶极矩和电四极矩，发现粲夸克对电磁矩在算符层面代数消失，且该特性可作为区分不同四夸克结构（如紧致态与分子态）的方法学标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分不同单重粲四夸克构型（如紧致态与分子态）的内部组织结构，寻找能反映其内在几何与自旋-电荷分布关系的可观测量。

Method: 在二夸克–反二夸克图像和轻锥QCD求和规则框架下，对所有允许的$u/d$味组合进行味分解，并分析电磁多极矩及其无量纲比值$\mathcal{D}/\mu$。

Result: 粲夸克对电磁矩在算符层面代数消失；磁矩随轻夸克味呈规律性符号变化（$uu$为正、$dd$为负、混合味受抑）；电四极矩始终非零，表明电荷分布本征变形；无量纲比值$\mathcal{D}/\mu$是与插值流归一化无关的结构可观测量。

Conclusion: 粲夸克贡献的代数解耦及其所揭示的算符级区分能力，构成了判别不同单重粲四夸克架构（包括紧致态与分子态）的关键方法学特征。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of the magnetic dipole and electric quadrupole moments of compact $[q_1 q_2][\bar q_3 \bar c]$ tetraquark states with quantum numbers $J^P = 1^+$, in the diquark--antidiquark picture and within the light-cone QCD sum rule framework. By performing a flavor decomposition across all admissible $u/d$ configurations, we show that the charm-quark contribution to the electromagnetic moments vanishes algebraically at the operator level -- a structural feature of the specific spin organization adopted here, in which the heavy antiquark resides in a pseudoscalar antidiquark, distinct from the dynamical $1/m_c$ suppression operative in alternative compact constructions and in hadronic molecules. The magnetic moments follow a coherent light-flavor pattern -- positive for $uu$-, negative for $dd$-, and suppressed for mixed-flavor diquarks -- and the quadrupole moments are non-zero throughout, corresponding, in the customary geometrical interpretation, to intrinsically deformed charge distributions. We further identify the dimensionless multipole ratio $\mathcal{D}/μ$ as a structural observable, independent of the normalization of the interpolating current, which probes the relative geometry of the spin-current and charge distributions complementary to the absolute moments. Taken together, the algebraic decoupling of the charm contribution and the operator-level distinction it implies among different singly-heavy tetraquark architectures constitute methodological signatures that discriminate among the corresponding internal organizations and, to the extent that a physical candidate is dominated by one of them, among compact and molecular interpretations.

</details>


### [6] [Annihilation feedback on elastic scattering in self-interacting dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27056)
*Kalliopi Petraki,Thomas Pignard*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了暗物质湮灭对弹性散射的反馈效应，特别是在长程力存在的情况下。结果表明，在Sommerfeld共振附近，这种反馈会抑制低速下弹性截面的增长；而在弹性散射零点附近，则可能因幺正性要求而产生非零的弹性散射底值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由星系结构观测所启发，暗物质可能存在显著的自相互作用，这常源于轻的力媒介子交换；而轻媒介子可能导致散射非微扰并使非弹性过程变得重要。

Method: 基于幺正性（光学定理），分析弹性与非弹性相互作用之间的耦合关系，研究暗物质湮灭对弹性散射振幅的反馈效应，尤其关注长程力增强下的Sommerfeld共振区域。

Result: 暗物质湮灭的反馈在Sommerfeld共振附近显著抑制低速弹性截面增长；在弹性散射零点附近，可因幺正性要求诱导出非零的弹性散射底值。

Conclusion: 弹性与非弹性过程不可独立处理，湮灭反馈对暗物质自相互作用建模至关重要，尤其在共振与零点区域需纳入幺正性约束。

Abstract: Sizable dark-matter self-interactions, motivated by observations of galactic structure, often arise from the exchange of light force mediators. Light mediators can render scattering nonperturbative and make inelastic processes important. Unitarity, via the optical theorem, implies that elastic and inelastic interactions cannot be treated independently: inelasticity induces absorptive contributions to the two-particle self-energy kernel that determines the elastic amplitude, while the resulting incoming-state dynamics also modifies the inelastic amplitudes. We study the feedback of dark-matter annihilation on elastic scattering in the presence of a long-range force. Although annihilation is a short-distance process, it can be strongly enhanced by such a force. We find that its feedback becomes significant near Sommerfeld resonances, where it suppresses the low-velocity growth of the baseline elastic cross section. Near baseline elastic-scattering zeros, it may instead generate a nonzero elastic floor required by unitarity. A companion paper studies the feedback of radiative bound-state formation on elastic scattering, which can generate sizable self-interactions.

</details>


### [7] [Probing Lepton Flavor Violation at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27057)
*L. Allwicher,R. Bartocci,M. Cepeda,C. Cornella,L. P. S. Leal,A. Lusiani,C. Martin Perez,P. Paradisi,E. Scantamburlo,O. Sumensari*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在FCC-ee对撞机Z极点运行下，利用约2×10¹¹个τ轻子对探测轻子味破坏（LFV）的潜力，发现τ衰变过程（如τ→3ℓ和τ→ℓγ）在某些SMEFT算符上可超越Z→τℓ和h→τℓ等传统探针的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对未来正负电子对撞机LFV研究多集中于Z→τℓ和h→τℓ过程，而τ轻子稀有衰变在高统计量下可能提供更强或互补的LFV探针，尤其在SMEFT框架下需系统评估其相对敏感度。

Method: 基于FCC-ee Z极点预期的2×10¹¹ τ对事例数，计算τ→3ℓ和τ→ℓγ（ℓ=e,μ）分支比的灵敏度提升；对比Z→τℓ和h→τℓ过程的现有与预计极限；在SMEFT框架下分析不同类型LFV算符（Yukawa型与半轻子型）对各过程的贡献机制与灵敏度差异。

Result: τ衰变对多数LFV算符的灵敏度优于Z→τℓ，且覆盖更广SMEFT算符空间；对Yukawa型LFV相互作用，h→τℓ仍最强；但对能辐射诱导LFV希格斯衰变的半轻子算符，τ衰变反成主导探针。

Conclusion: τ轻子稀有衰变是FCC-ee上探测LFV的重要且不可替代的通道，尤其在SMEFT中能提供对特定新物理算符的独特、甚至最优约束，应被纳入未来LFV整体研究策略。

Abstract: We investigate the prospects for probing Lepton Flavor Violation (LFV) at FCC-ee within the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) framework. The approximately $2 \times 10^{11}$ $τ$-lepton pairs expected at the $Z$-pole run of FCC-ee could improve current limits on the branching fractions of $τ\to3\ell$ and $τ\to\ellγ$ ($\ell=e,μ$) by factors ranging from 30 to 800, depending on the specific channel. We compare the resulting new-physics reach with that obtained from searches for $Z \to τ\ell$ and $h \to τ\ell$, which have so far received greater attention in studies of LFV at future $e^+e^-$ colliders. We find that $τ$ decays can surpass both the current and the projected sensitivity of $Z\toτ\ell$ searches to the same LFV interactions, while probing a broader range of SMEFT operators. We further show that, for LFV Yukawa-like interactions, direct searches for $h\toτ\ell$ provide the strongest sensitivity. By contrast, for semileptonic operators that radiatively induce LFV Higgs decays, this hierarchy can be reversed, with $τ$ decays providing the leading probe.

</details>


### [8] [A Natural Dark Matter-Dark Energy Interaction from Axion-Monopole Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27059)
*Hengameh Bagherian,Matthew Reece,Lian-Tao Wang,Huangyu Xiao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于暗规范场中轴子-磁单极子动力学的暗物质与暗能量相互作用新框架，其中暗物质为't Hooft-Polyakov磁单极子，暗能量为超轻轴子；通过Witten效应诱导轴子依赖的带电单极子（dyon）质量，实现直接耦合；模型利用单值性塔（monodromy tower）指数压低量子修正以解决自然性问题；预测耦合强度约0.1%，可提升至百分比量级以解释表观幻影暗能量，并能同时拟合DESI数据和宇宙双折射现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决暗物质与暗能量强耦合模型中普遍存在的Coleman-Weinberg势远大于暗能量标度的自然性问题，并构建一个能同时解释DESI观测与宇宙双折射的自洽理论框架。

Method: 构建含暗规范场、超轻轴子及't Hooft-Polyakov磁单极子的暗部门模型；利用Witten效应引入轴子依赖的dyon质量以实现暗物质-暗能量直接相互作用；通过monodromy塔机制抑制量子修正；结合宇宙学观测（DESI、宇宙双折射）约束参数空间。

Result: 模型可指数压低Coleman-Weinberg修正，使~0.1%的耦合强度自然可行；增强高荷dyon丰度后耦合可达百分比量级，导致表观w < -1的幻影暗能量行为；DESI偏好参数区间可同时自然解释小异常系数的宇宙双折射。

Abstract: We propose a natural framework for interacting dark matter and dark energy based on axion-monopole dynamics in a dark gauge sector. Experimental searches for non-gravitational DM interactions have largely targeted DM with GeV-TeV masses via nuclear recoils, yielding no discovery thus far. DM in the MeV-GeV mass range, however, is theoretically motivated, reproducing the observed relic abundance while remaining invisible to nuclear recoil searches. We also simulate DM signatures at the next-generation experiment SHiP to assess its sensitivity. Within minimal DM models, we present a likelihood-based statistical framework for LDMX using the full two-dimensional recoil electron distribution, spanning exclusion, discovery, parameter estimation, and model comparison in frequentist and Bayesian formulations. Beyond this paradigm, we perform global fits of scalar and fermionic light DM, combining cosmological, astrophysical, and laboratory constraints. We then extend fixed-target searches to spin-1 DM and higher electromagnetic moment dark photons, yielding rich signatures with strong complementarity to other probes. Finally, we go beyond standard nuclear modelling of dark photon production, incorporating elastic nuclear form factors and spectral functions from many-body ab initio methods in place of analytic treatments.

We used these line detections and similar from ALPINE to calibrate UV luminosity-$L_{\mathrm{[CII]}}$ and stellar mass-$L_{\mathrm{[CII]}}$ relations at $z\sim7$. Given the challenge in precisely defining a selection volume for the REBELS sample itself, we rely on published UV LF and stellar mass function (SMF) determinations at $z\sim7$ and three complementary approaches to derive the [CII] LF. The UV LF-based method provides a lower limit on the [CII] LF, due to its missing the most dust-obscured systems. Our SMF-based estimate yields a Schechter function with a characteristic luminosity of $L^*_{\mathrm{[CII]}}=10^{9.11^{+0.11}_{-0.27}} L_\odot$ and a steep faint-end slope. Our results indicate a general underprediction from models of the abundance of [CII] emitters at high-redshift and thus potentially the number densities of massive yet UV-faint systems implied by our SMF-based method. Additionally, they suggest that 63% of the star formation rate density occurs in massive [CII]-bright systems and that $\sim$60-90% of [CII] emitters at $z\sim7$ may be missed by bright UV selections. This highlights the importance of surveys focusing on especially massive, UV-faint sources at $z>6$.

Abstract: We develop a parametric toy model to explore how black hole mass scatter $σ_{\rm BH}$ and the dependence of the quenching probability per unit time on black hole mass, parameterized as a power law with index $q$, jointly control the faint-end slope $α$ of the stellar mass function of quenched central galaxies. By varying $σ_{\rm BH}$ and $q$, we identify two regimes. At low scatter, $σ_{\rm BH} \lesssim 0.4$ dex, $α$ is strongly sensitive to $q$. Gradual quenching, represented by low $q$, produces shallow slopes, while sharp thresholds, with high $q$, produce steep mass functions, making $α$ a powerful diagnostic of AGN feedback prescriptions. At large scatter, $σ_{\rm BH} \gtrsim 0.6$ dex, models with vastly different $q$ converge to similar quenched mass functions, because the intrinsic scatter erases the imprint of the feedback implementation. Comparing to cosmological hydrodynamical simulations and semianalytic models, only models with $σ_{\rm BH} \gtrsim 0.4$ dex match the observed faint-end slope of $α\approx 0.7$. {\sc SAGE}, {\sc Dark Sage}, and {\sc COLIBRE}, with $σ_{\rm BH}$ of 2.0, 0.6, and 0.4 dex, respectively, reproduce this slope, whereas {\sc IllustrisTNG} and {\sc EAGLE}, with $σ_{\rm BH} \lesssim 0.2$ dex, produce steeper slopes. Current observational estimates of $σ_{\rm BH}$ allow a wide range of $q \gtrsim 0.8$, so the observed slope alone cannot identify the quenching prescription. If $σ_{\rm BH}$ is small, simultaneous measurements of $α$ and $σ_{\rm BH}$ can distinguish between competing AGN feedback models.

Abstract: Understanding how stars form at low metallicity is challenging because carbon monoxide (CO) emission, which usually probes the molecular gas reservoir, is faint, and tends to be concentrated in parsec-scale clumps. To overcome this difficulty, in order to study the molecular clouds in metal-poor environments, we have acquired CO(2-1) ALMA maps at 3-4 pc resolution of four nearby dwarf galaxies with nebular (O/H) metallicities ranging from roughly 1/9 Z_sun to 1/3 Z_sun. Significant CO signal in the cubes has been identified through two masks, a ``strict'', high-confidence, mask and a ``broad'' one for better completeness. Abstract: Understanding gas accretion and feedback in low-mass galaxy groups bridges the gap between isolated ellipticals and cluster-dominant galaxies. We present new Multi Unit Spectroscopic Explorer (MUSE) observations of 17 nearby brightest group galaxies (BGGs), revealing diverse origins for their gas reservoirs. Warm ionized gas, traced by Halpha and [N II], is detected in 77% (13/17) of the sample, with morphologies including rotating disks with or without tails and diffuse emission, rings, and filaments. Unlike brightest cluster galaxies, most BGGs host regularly rotating stars, whereas their gas displays diverse kinematics. Nearly half of the rotating gas disks are aligned with the stars, consistent with internal recycling. However, tails, diffuse emission, or rings in three aligned systems may retain signatures of past accretion or interactions. The remaining disks, ring-like structures, and most filamentary systems are misaligned, supporting gas acquisition through mergers, interactions, or halo cooling. Compact disks are consistent with centrally concentrated, low-angular-momentum gas, possibly linked to residual cooling. Most filamentary systems lie near the precipitation threshold (tcool/teddy ~ 1-2) and preferentially occupy the turbulence-dominated region of the k-plot, supporting condensation consistent with chaotic cold accretion. All galaxies with detected X-ray halos, including those without warm gas, exhibit low central entropies (<30 keV cm^2) and short central cooling times (<3 Gyr). These conditions alone cannot predict multiphase gas, suggesting that AGN- or interaction-driven perturbations and turbulence-induced thermal instabilities help regulate condensation. No single mechanism dominates: BGGs are transitional systems in which internal recycling, environmental accretion, and cooling-driven condensation coexist, linking isolated ellipticals to cluster-dominant galaxies.

Abstract: The G4Jy Sample consists of 1,863 of the brightest sources in the southern sky, as selected at 151 MHz using the GaLactic and Extragalactic All-sky MWA (GLEAM) survey. We supplement existing, low-frequency radio data by completing visual inspection of radio images from the Rapid ASKAP Continuum Survey (RACS) at 887.5, 1367.5 and 1655.5 MHz. Doing so on a source-by-source basis allows us to assess the quality of the association of the radio emission, which we indicate via a flag system. Having multiple radio flux-densities allows us to construct broadband radio spectral energy distributions (SEDs), and these extend up to 20 GHz for sources that also have data from the Australia Telescope Compact Array (ATCA). We fit polynomial functions to these SEDs, and determine the distribution of sources that are best-fit by absorption models, these accounting for 11 per cent of the sample. The SEDs are further modelled using the Radio AGN in Semi-analytic Environments (RAiSE) framework, for three assumed density-profiles (set by typical halo masses of 10^12.5, 10^13.5, and 10^14.5 Msol). The derived source ages show the expected distribution in the radio-power--size (P--D) diagram, whilst derived jet powers show non-linear behaviour. The latter is believed to be the result of higher densities, associated with higher magnetic fields, restricting the expansion of the radio jets/lobes.

It is selected as the best descriptor for a clear majority of the sample (60.6\%) and, critically, also achieves the best median goodness-of-fit, with a reduced chi-squared of $χ^2_ν = 0.41$. In contrast, the standard NFW framework is preferred in only 12.6\% of cases, while the MOND and Freeman disk models are selected as optimal for just 10.3\% and 9.1\%, respectively. No field shows statistically compelling kinematic evidence for a distinct system after accounting for the multiple candidates examined. Minni 329 shows the strongest spatial excess, but this is not significant after multiple-testing correction and is not accompanied by significant central concentration. The KMOS targets predominantly trace the Sgr population, but Sgr compatibility alone does not confirm the individual candidates as distinct stellar clusters. None of the sixteen fields can be confirmed as a separate stellar system with the present data, although this does not establish that the original candidates are spurious. Clump-detection completeness is quantified through injection-recovery simulations, which we use to define ($M_\star$, $z$)-dependent 50 per cent completeness-limited $f_{\rm clumpy}$, alongside a probability-weighted completeness-corrected statistic for comparison. Most clumpy galaxies host only one or two robustly detected off-centre clumps, while higher-multiplicity systems occur preferentially in massive, strongly star-forming galaxies at low to intermediate redshift. Our results are consistent with a transition from merger-dominated clumpy morphologies at early times and low stellar masses to an increasing contribution from in situ clump-formation channels, such as disc instabilities, in more massive systems.

Abstract: Dwarf galaxies are abundant and dark matter dominated, rendering them particularly relevant for testing a cosmological model dominated by a dark sector. Whether their density profiles agree with $Λ$CDM expectations has long been a point of contention, dubbed the cusp/core problem. While numerical simulations have suggested that the erasure of central dark matter cusps may arise from potential fluctuations in feedback-driven gas, whether the required fluctuation levels are achieved in actual galaxies has remained uncertain. By measuring the power spectra of neutral gas density contrasts in a sample of gas-rich dwarf galaxies, we show that the observed fluctuations match theoretical expectations when their impact is derived from a stochastic dynamical model. Specifically, the relaxation times for core formation at the observationally estimated radii are generally on the order of a Hubble time or less. Core sizes are therefore consistent with conservative estimates assuming their generation through steady fluctuations acting over cosmic history. The relaxation time increases with halo mass, suggesting that core formation via this mechanism becomes less efficient in more massive systems.

Conclusion: MIR与FIR联合诊断对于准确恢复ISM物理参数的径向趋势至关重要；本工作为星暴与AGN宿主星系中气体与尘埃的空间分辨研究提供了基准。

Abstract: We investigate how star formation and active galactic nuclei (AGNs) shape the ionized and neutral interstellar medium in 67 nearby luminous and ultraluminous infrared galaxies from the Great Observatories All-sky LIRG Survey (GOALS). Combining mid-infrared (MIR) spectral mapping from the Spitzer Infrared Spectrograph with far-infrared (FIR) spectroscopy from the Herschel Photodetector Array Camera and Spectrometer, we measure fine-structure emission lines in aperture grids extending to projected galactocentric distances of about 14 kpc. We compare the measurements with CLOUDY photoionization models to constrain the ionization parameter, hydrogen density, stellar age, and metallicity. In starburst-dominated galaxies, the combined MIR+FIR fits yield a decreasing ionization parameter with radius, approximately constant hydrogen densities, uniformly young stellar populations, and flat metallicity profiles. MIR-only fits instead yield an increasing ionization parameter with radius, highlighting the importance of FIR diagnostics, particularly the [C II] emission associated with photodissociation regions, in constraining the multiphase gas. In the strongest AGN-dominated system, the nucleus and inner extended regions show elevated [Ne III]/[Ne II] and suppressed [C II]/[O I] ratios, while the outermost regions, at projected distances of about 6 kpc and beyond, show values typical of star-forming regions. This suggests that the AGN may affect gas excitation out to about 5 kpc. Our results demonstrate the importance of combining MIR and FIR diagnostics to recover radial trends that can be hidden in galaxy-integrated measurements. Together with Paper I, they provide a benchmark for spatially resolved studies of gas and dust in starbursts and AGN-hosting galaxies.

Abstract: We investigate spatial variations in polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon (PAH) properties and photoelectric heating efficiency in 67 nearby luminous infrared galaxies from the Great Observatories All-sky LIRG Survey (GOALS). Using mid-infrared spectroscopy from Spitzer and far-infrared cooling-line maps from Herschel, we extract spectra in concentric annuli and matched aperture grids out to about 14 kpc. PAH features are measured with the CAFE spectral decomposition tool. We trace photoelectric heating efficiency by combining PAH emission with cooling lines after isolating the neutral-gas contribution. In starburst galaxies, the PAH 11.3/7.7 ratio rises out to about 5 kpc while 6.2/7.7 remains nearly constant, indicating predominantly large, neutral PAHs. Between 5 and 8 kpc, 11.3/7.7 continues to rise while inferred PAH sizes decrease. Beyond 8 kpc, 11.3/7.7 falls and 6.2/7.7 rises, indicating smaller, more ionized PAHs. These trends are consistent with PAH photodestruction near the central starburst. Strong active galactic nucleus (AGN) systems show more neutral PAHs and harder radiation fields in their nuclear and inner regions. Beyond about 5 kpc, PAHs become more ionized and approach the starburst locus as the radiation field weakens. Strong AGNs influence average PAH charge states at 6-14 kpc as effectively as moderate AGNs at 1-6 kpc. Photoelectric heating efficiency increases with radius in both starburst and AGN-hosting galaxies as the radiation field weakens. In starbursts, higher efficiency is associated with more neutral PAHs, while AGN-dominated systems show the opposite relation. These results provide quantitative, spatially resolved constraints on the extent of AGN effects on dust properties in a population of luminous infrared galaxies.

TL;DR: 本文综述了图神经网络（GNN）在天体物理与宇宙学中的应用，强调其天然适配物理系统内在几何结构、可融合先验物理知识、具备更高可解释性，并指出当前多数研究仍局限于模拟数据而非真实观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统本质上是关系型的（如星系与其环境、粒子在探测器中的时空关联），而图结构能自然刻画这类关系；传统深度学习模型（如CNN、Transformer）难以兼顾物理对称性与几何先验，GNN则更契合物理建模需求。

Method: 基于几何深度学习原理，从物理第一性原理出发构建图表示与GNN架构；将CNN和Transformer视为统一几何框架下的特例；系统梳理GNN在宇宙学、星系形成、动力学及仪器数据处理中的实际应用。

Result: 发现除仪器领域外，仅约1/6的论文将GNN应用于真实观测数据；总结出一套GNN应用的最佳实践指南，包括明确何时不宜使用图结构。

Conclusion: GNN是目前最契合物理建模本质的深度学习范式之一，兼具理论合理性、物理可解释性与工程灵活性，但需推动其向真实数据驱动的研究范式转型。

Abstract: Physics seldom makes breakthroughs when we consider objects only in isolation. Instead we model and measure how objects sit among their neighbours, spatial, temporal, or otherwise: a galaxy in its environment, a halo in its history, a photon hit in a detector. Graphs are ideally suited for encoding exactly these relations, and Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) learn the geometric functions that respect them. This review presents the use of GNNs for astrophysics and cosmology, and argues that GNNs often suit physics and physical data better than any alternative: they fit the native geometry of the domain and the signal, enable the incorporation of the symmetries and prior knowledge we already trust and have toiled to build, and stay flexible enough to capture what we do not yet understand. GNNs' physically intuitive workings also render them more interpretable than other deep-learning architectures. We show how we can begin from general principles of the geometry of deep learning and build the graph and the graph neural network from the ground up, and how other architectures, like the convolutional network and the transformer, can be seen as special cases of one unified geometric blueprint. We survey applications across cosmology, galaxy formation, dynamics, and instrumentation; and we chart the frontier. From this survey, we find that only one in six articles outside instrumentation applies GNNs to data rather than just simulations. We also distil a set of best practices, including when not to reach for a graph. Throughout, we will prioritise physical intuition and foundational principles over mathematical machinery whenever possible.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能（AI）如何可能引发信息公地（scientific literature as an information commons）的‘圈地运动’，类比历史上英国农业公地被私有化的过程，指出AI正通过数据攫取、模型训练与内容生成（如‘slop spam’）等方式，将共享知识转化为私人资本，威胁支撑公共知识生态的社会系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在警示AI技术正在催生一种新型‘信息圈地运动’，其本质不是简单的数据滥用，而是对人类知识共享根基的系统性侵蚀，需从政治经济学角度重新审视AI发展路径。

Method: 采用历史类比与政治经济学分析框架，将当代AI实践与17–19世纪英国农业‘圈地运动’进行结构性对照，并引入‘slop spam’等新概念阐释AI驱动的知识私有化机制。

Result: 提出AI-enabled enclosure这一核心论点：AI并非仅造成短期伦理失范，而是具备重构知识产权结构、瓦解公共知识生态的能力；信息公地虽未像农业公地那样物理消失，但其公共性正被算法逻辑悄然掏空。

Conclusion: AI的发展必须置于公共治理与制度设计的框架下，否则将重演圈地悲剧——不是资源耗尽，而是公共资源被系统性私有化，最终危及人类文明赖以存续的知识共享方式。

Abstract: The scientific literature can be viewed as an "information commons:" an intangible resource that "no one owns and is shared by everyone." There are well-established economic and political frameworks for understanding such commons, emerging from the history of agricultural common lands found in places like preindustrial England. This article provides a primer on the history and these frameworks, and considers a key difference between the information commons and the fields of yore: while information commons are robust and immensely valuable --- some can be said to rank among humanity's greatest achievements --- the English agricultural commons no longer meaningfully exist. The latter are gone not because of the well-known "tragedy of the commons," but rather a phenomenon known as the enclosure movement (~1600--1800) that transformed the common lands into private property. Many observers, then and now, judge enclosure to have been theft at a societal scale. This article argues that artificial intelligence (AI) provides precisely the tools needed to enable a previously-infeasible enclosure of the information commons. Although AI-enabled enclosure does not operate identically to physical enclosure, it shares the key features of turning the commons into private wealth and undermining the social systems that support them (via "slop spam"). The concepts presented in this article can provide a scholarly basis for interrogating the foreboding feeling that AI may represent not just a smash-and-grab, but an attack upon a whole way of life.

</details>

Abstract: The selection of candidates belonging to rare or underrepresented stellar populations, as well as novel objects, is essential for spectroscopic follow-up. In this work, we demonstrate how combining broad- and narrow-band photometric information with machine-learning techniques enables this selection. Using photometric data in twelve filters from the Javalambre Photometric Local Universe Survey (J-PLUS) as input, we applied an anomaly detection technique based on autoencoders to identify spectral energy distributions (SEDs) that deviate from the dominant pattern in the J-PLUS objects sample. Subsequently, we applied the t-distributed stochastic neighbor embedding (t-SNE) algorithm to the J-PLUS colors of the outlier SEDs population. Combining information from the SIMBAD database and other astronomical surveys to classify the outliers identified by the autoencoder, we are able to characterize over-densities in the t-SNE map. Our study specifically focuses on well-defined islands in the t-SNE space, corresponding to specific populations. We identified a large sample of white dwarfs and low-mass stars, including a small subset of polluted white dwarfs, and report potential candidates for spectroscopic follow-up, including 19 carbon-rich stars, 11 cataclysmic variables and 170 white-dwarfs-main-sequence binaries, all previously unreported in the literature.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在航天器、粒子加速器等高辐射环境中部署时，易受高能粒子影响导致权重数据持续退化，传统离线训练假设失效，需提升推理阶段对比特翻转的容错能力。

Method: 扩展Fault-Aware Training（FAT）范式，在训练中引入对齐惩罚项，使未损坏的激活和权重聚集于低秩子空间；运行时检测并标记被破坏的坐标，再从完好坐标中重建修复。

Result: 在六个回归基准上验证，固定FAT检查点仅启用运行时修复，可进一步降低约23%原单位过量积分误差；相比经典SECDED编码，中位数下操作清刷间隔分别提升2.04×（RMSE+5%）和1.96×（RMSE+50%）。

Conclusion: Projection Healing为构建高韧性数据驱动模型提供了新路径，可与硬件冗余、ECC及FAT等既有技术互补。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) models are increasingly being deployed into radiation-challenging environments in spacecraft, particle accelerators, and, soon, space-based data centers. Instantiated as a learned orchestration of numbers stored in persistent memory, models are susceptible to continual degradation as high-energy particles corrupt data. At inference time, new data passed through the network fundamentally break the off-line assumptions and guarantees of a static model determined during training. To preserve the quality and efficacy of the models, fault tolerance against bit flips is a clear objective. In this work we developed a runtime method for repairing the quantized weights of neural networks (NNs) after bit flips on commodity, commercial off-the-shelf (COTS) hardware, without storing extra replicas. Our work extends the well-established Fault-Aware Training (FAT) paradigm by adding an alignment penalty to concentrate undamaged activations and weights into a low-rank subspace, and we then reconstruct flagged corrupted coordinates from the uncorrupted survivors. We call this method {\it Projection Healing} and apply it to six regression benchmarks on quantized NNs. Holding a FAT checkpoint fixed and toggling only the runtime heal gives a further ${\sim}23\%$ reduction in the native-unit excess integral. Across those datasets the method extends the operational scrub interval by a median of $2.04\times$ at a $+5\%$ RMSE degradation and $1.96\times$ at $+50\%$, compared with $1.62\times$ and $1.07\times$ for classical Single Error Correction, Double Error Detection (SECDED). This introduces a new path towards designing resilient data-driven models, complementing established hardware redundancy, ECC, and FAT-based techniques.

</details>

Abstract: Widefield single-dish telescopes operating at (sub-)mm wavelengths ($λ\sim 4$~mm to 350~$μ$m) tend to feature much faster mapping speeds than radio interferometers at the same wavelengths, and are more suitable for measuring signals on larger spatial scales. However, the data are dominated by atmospheric fluctuations on large scales and by photon and instrumental noise on smaller scales, making it difficult to recover the true astronomical sky. We build on maria-nifty, a Gaussian process-based Bayesian imaging framework for reconstructing sky maps from single-dish telescope data, implemented on top of Numerical Information Field Theory (NIFTy). It uses modular generative models to efficiently separate the astronomical signal from atmospheric emission while providing pixel-wise uncertainty quantification for the reconstructed sky maps. Moreover, our method cleanly separates individual astrophysical signals, even though they are superposed on the sky. Following its successful validation on synthetic time-ordered data generated using the maria software, we apply it to real data observed with the MUSTANG-2 bolometric array on the 100-meter Green Bank Telescope. For the galaxy cluster RXJ1347.5-1145, we separate the extended cluster emission from the compact source at its centre and obtain a smoother map with fewer artefacts compared to a standard maximum-likelihood reconstruction using the same data. The JAX-based implementation and GPU acceleration provide the basis for applying the method to the vastly larger datasets that the next generation of single-dish telescopes will deliver.

</details>

Abstract: The noise properties of an InGaAs camera vary with gain, requiring a trade-off between implementation complexity and precision. Using generalized least squares (GLS) with known covariance as the precision benchmark, we examine the applicability and precision penalties of ordinary least squares (OLS) and correlated double sampling (CDS) at different noise operating points. We define $η=fT/σ_\mathrm{R}^2$, where $f$ is the total pixel count rate, $T$ the fitting span, and $σ_\mathrm{R}$ the single-read noise. For constant flux, uniform sampling, and independent white read noise plus Poisson noise, established finite-sampling variance expressions give equal OLS and CDS variances at $η=10$ for $N\ge4$ reads; OLS is favored below this boundary and CDS above it. Analytical and Monte Carlo comparisons for three gain modes quantify the precision loss of the better simplified estimator relative to GLS. In high-gain on-sky data, choosing OLS at 20 s and CDS at 90 s increases the background residual standard deviation by 3.55% and 3.01%, respectively, relative to two-iteration feasible GLS (FGLS). Photometry of 21 common sources does not clearly resolve most estimator differences. The observed OLS-CDS crossover occurs earlier than the ideal prediction, indicating that temporal covariance beyond the ideal model also affects the selection boundary. Observational validation is limited to high gain; medium- and low-gain choices rely on theory and simulations using calibrated parameters. OLS or CDS can replace GLS with a small precision penalty at suitable operating points. The ideal criterion provides an initial selection rule, whose practical boundary requires validation against observed temporal covariance.

We do not repeat in detail the important background these Chapters provide but encourage the reader to review them before reading this Chapter. With the SKAO, we should push the explored parameter space further and in new directions in all of the sensitivity, field-of-view, time, spectral, spectro-dynamic, polarisation and imaging domains. We should utilise approaches such as machine learning in allowing us to address the complexity that arises from the expanded parameter space and increased data rates and candidate sources. Abstract: The NSF-DOE Vera C. Rubin Observatory will complete its ten-year Legacy Survey of Space and Time (LSST) in the mid-2030s. Its 8.4 m aperture and 9.6 square degree field of view give it an étendue unmatched by any facility planned for that era. The Rubin 2036: Opportunities for Discovery workshop (KIPAC/SLAC, 3-5 August 2026) brought together approximately 100 participants to discuss Rubin's future after LSST. This paper summarizes the workshop's structure and discussions across five working groups. The continued-surveying group explored a machine-learning foundation model pretrained on Rubin imaging for detection, deblending, and classification. The cadence group considered allocating post-LSST observing time and strategies for transiting exoplanets, Type Ia supernova cosmology, time-delay cosmography, and second-scale transients. The filter group explored broadband, medium-band, and narrowband filters for photometric redshifts, high-redshift large-scale structure, AGN reverberation mapping, Galactic archaeology, and emission-line imaging. The multi-messenger group explored follow-up of next-generation gravitational-wave and neutrino sources, including standard sirens, kilonovae, and Galactic supernovae. The technology group organized options by cost and lead time, including fast readout, new filters, a faster shutter, and summit edge computing. Cross-cutting topics included AI/ML, Chilean and Latin-American co-leadership, satellite megaconstellations, and weather and climate trends at Cerro Pachón. White papers and workshop talks are available through https://zenodo.org/communities/rubin2036workshop/ and https://rubin-observatory.github.io/Rubin-Beyond-LSST/pages/Rubin2036proceedings/.

Abstract: Precision spectroscopy underpins quantum sensing, atomic timekeeping, and the search for terrestrial-mass exoplanets. Finding an Earth-Sun analog requires fractional splitting of spectral features that rivals the precision of the best laboratory spectroscopy. This corresponds to resolution of a few parts in $10^5$ of a typical stellar spectral line, or a Doppler velocity precision of less than 10 cm s$^{-1}$. Only a few astrophysical spectrometers reach this regime, and only by dispersing starlight with large gratings and averaging thousands of lines. Here, we show that comparable precision can be achieved on a single solar line by leveraging techniques used in quantum sensors and optical clocks. A laser is frequency-locked to the 1565 nm solar Fe I line by coupling to a single mode of the solar thermal field and counted against an optical frequency comb referenced to the SI second, thus creating a solar optical clock. Operating at the fundamental photon-noise limit of thermal heterodyne spectroscopy, we achieve centroid precision of one part in $10^9$ of the optical frequency, or 30 cm s$^{-1}$ in radial velocity. We recover the Sun's five-minute acoustic oscillations and the expected Rossiter-McLaughlin signal during a solar eclipse. Combining these techniques from quantum sensing with a thirty-meter-class telescope could enable our demonstrated precision on the nearest Sun-like stars in a few hours. Multiplexing such absolute frequency measurements across lines with different magnetic sensitivity and formation depth would reduce averaging time and help disentangle stellar granulation and activity from the Doppler signature of terrestrial exoplanets, providing new approaches to address some of the most demanding measurements in astrophysical spectroscopy.

</details>

Abstract: We present a summary of lessons learned from our community on the design and construction of infrared multi-object spectrographs. As members of the Magellan Infrared Multi-Object Spectrograph (MIRMOS) development team, we were motivated to learn about the most common troubles faced by our colleagues and incorporate measures in our design and assembly plan to address known issues. The lessons are compiled as a result of interviews we carried out with members of the instrument development teams for several multi-object infrared spectrographs. We found considerable overlap in the lessons learned for building this type of instrument; many of which relate to the cryogenic operation of the instruments. In this paper, we will present the compilation of general and specific lessons which we found relevant for the successful development of MIRMOS or any other future cryogenic instruments.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Padmanabhan和Kolekar在二维自由场中发现的递归热性现象至更一般的共形场论代数结构。

Method: 利用Bisognano-Wichmann性质、楔形区域的模结构及其因果伴生区域，通过共形映射逐级构造父子楔形，并分析对应代数与态的热性。

Result: 证明了每一代中父楔形态在限制到右子楔形时均为KMS态；识别出伴生代数为模交换子；显式导出递归模流。

Conclusion: 递归热性是共形场论中楔形代数结构的普适代数特征，不依赖于具体场内容，由模理论和共形对称性决定。

Abstract: Padmanabhan and Kolekar had demonstrated the phenomenon of recursive thermality in two dimensional free field theories where the Rindler vacuum appears thermal to an observer accelerated with respect to the Rindler observer. We study the same construction at an algebraic level for conformal field theories.
  At each step, a parent wedge is conformally mapped to a reference Minkowski spacetime, where a new right Rindler wedge and its left sibling are selected. We illustrate that under the Bisognano--Wichmann property, thermality holds at every generation: the state associated with a parent wedge becomes a KMS state when restricted to its right child. Further, using the modular structure of the wedge and its causal sibling, we identify the sibling algebra with the modular commutant and derive the corresponding recursive modular flow explicitly.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算量子费舍尔矩阵和几何张量的理论形式过于晦涩，限制了对其物理行为（尤其在基态）的直观理解；而具有连续对称性的系统已有基于守恒流和荷关联函数的模型无关、直观描述框架，亟需建立二者之间的桥梁。

Method: 利用具有连续对称性的量子系统的基态性质，将量子费舍尔矩阵与Nambu-Goldstone玻色子（由破缺生成元产生）进行对比分析；进一步将信息度量（费舍尔矩阵定义）与Zamolodchikov度量、Uhlmann曲率与电荷密度矩阵分别对应，并通过多个实例验证，最后推广至有限温度情形。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了量子计量学中的核心几何量（费舍尔矩阵、Uhlmann曲率）与对称性破缺理论中的基本物理对象（Nambu-Goldstone模式、Zamolodchikov度量、电荷密度），为理解多参数量子估计与低能有效理论提供了新的几何视角和物理直觉。

Abstract: The quantum Fisher matrix and geometric tensor are powerful tools for quantifying the nature of quantum systems. Unfortunately, the formalism needed to compute these quantities is opaque and has limited the development of physical intuition for their behavior, even in the ground state. For quantum systems with continuous symmetries, we know the ground state can be understood in a model-independent and intuitive way from the correlation functions of conserved currents and charges. In this paper, we demonstrate an equivalence between the quantum Fisher matrix associated with the ground states of systems with continuous symmetries and the Nambu-Goldstone. bosons produced by broken generators. We will show that the information metric defined by the Fisher matrix is the same as the Zamolodchikov metric that defines the coset space of the system. Next we show that the Uhlmann curvature associated with multi-parameter estimation is the same as the charge density matrix that defines the difference between type-A and type-B Nambu-Goldstone bosons. We demonstrate these connections in several examples and discuss the generalization to finite temperature.

Abstract: We study classical soliton solutions in split-signature anti-self-dual Yang-Mills theory. Abstract: We develop a topological method to detect classical flux vacua of M-theory on Calabi-Yau fourfolds with a single complex structure modulus using only asymptotic period data and monodromy information. To this end, we express the index of the F-term of the flux superpotential as a winding number along the boundary of the universal cover of the complex structure moduli space. The index evaluates to a sum over zeros and poles of a suitably normalized boundary F-term, where local contributions can be computed algebraically on a dense set of boundary points. Assuming a conjectured bound on the remaining contributions, sufficiently many sign changes of the boundary F-term guarantee a flux vacuum in the interior. In general, this property does not hold for holomorphic (4,0)-periods.

</details>

Conclusion: Veneziano振幅的极值性并非仅由基本正性决定，而是依赖于更精细的UV性质（如KLT软性），这为理解弦理论的独特性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Whether string amplitudes can be characterized by extremality within the space of consistent S-matrices is an intriguing question. We address this question within the framework of positivity bootstrap. Two general requirements are emphasized: the scalar must lie in a near-false-vacuum (slow-roll--like) regime, and the exterior is driven to the extremal Reissner--Nordström--AdS locus in the thin-wall limit, in which the wall approaches the common would-be horizon. Since the solution is static and spherically symmetric, it may correspond to a semiclassical charge-$Q$ state of the dual CFT, realizing de~Sitter space approximately within a holographic theory. We also discuss free fluctuations whose de~Sitter--core modes appear as parametrically low-lying excitations above the state, bulk reconstruction, and the near-extremal regime, where finite-${\mathcal N}$ effects are expected to cut off the formal classical throat and replace its independent free-field modes by an interacting near-throat sector.

Abstract: The trans-Planckian censorship conjecture (TCC) limits inflation to a deadline of $N_{\rm TCC}=\ln(M_{\rm Pl}/H_{\rm end})$ e-folds. We formulate it in stochastic inflation as a bound at confidence $1-δ$ on the probability that a history outlasts the deadline, with three readings: one worldline, the region grown from one Hubble patch, and the reheated volume. Each reading becomes a closed-form condition, rigorous for a linear roll in leading-order stochastic inflation (for the region, in a branching model) and sharp at leading order in $N_{\rm TCC}$: a linear roll whose classical history lasts $N_{\rm cl}$ e-folds respects the TCC if $1-N_{\rm cl}/N_{\rm TCC}\geq\{2\mathcal{P}_ζ[w+\ln(1/δ)/N_{\rm TCC}]\}^{1/2}$, with $w=0$ for a worldline and $w=3$ for a region. On a linear slope without vacuum decay, the worldline and region readings are never weaker than the classical TCC, and on the CMB-normalized attractor all readings agree closely with it, so the bounds on the scale of inflation stand. A classical phase that ends in a flat quantum well, which the overdamped classical TCC excludes, is allowed with a margin of a few e-folds per worldline. Near the onset of eternal inflation, the readings differ. On a linear slope, a region must leave the fraction $\sqrt{6\mathcal{P}_ζ}$ of the deadline unused at leading order: eternal inflation is excluded for every classical history that uses a finite fraction of the deadline, as is a band of non-eternal slopes, and the slope bound becomes $|V'|>V^{3/2}/[\sqrt2\,π(1-N_{\rm cl}/N_{\rm TCC})]$. Per worldline, every slope is allowed, given a margin of a few standard deviations. A false vacuum must decay at about the eternal threshold $Γ=9H^4/(4π)$ or faster for a region or the volume, but only faster than $3H^4\ln(1/δ)/(4πN_{\rm TCC})$ per worldline. Whether the TCC forbids eternal inflation depends on the reading.

Abstract: We investigate bulk--boundary structures in $\AdS_4/\CFT_3$ from the viewpoint of gravity as a broken phase of conformal gauge symmetry. A crucial distinction is made between the Euler and Pontryagin characteristic classes. The gauge-theoretic gravitational action contains an Euler sector, whose total-derivative representation gives a boundary form depending on both intrinsic and extrinsic geometry. This structure is different from the Pontryagin descent to the three-dimensional gravitational Chern--Simons functional, whose metric variation gives the Cotton tensor and underlies the mixed boundary conditions studied by de Haro. We perform a Fefferman--Graham expansion of the Euler boundary form in asymptotically $\AdS_4$ spacetime. The normalizable coefficient $g_{(3)ij}$ does not contribute linearly to the finite Euler boundary term: its contribution from the cubic extrinsic- curvature sector vanishes by $\mathrm{Tr}\,g_{(3)}=0$, while curvature-- extrinsic-curvature terms containing $g_{(3)}$ vanish at the conformal boundary. A direct Codazzi analysis additionally shows that the Cotton tensor appears in the asymptotic mixed curvature but its contribution through the tangential-connection boundary variation vanishes for standard Dirichlet data. This indicates two distinct types of bulk--boundary structure: a global/topological Euler relation and a local Pontryagin--Cotton relation. We distinguish this kinematic Cotton appearance from a finite Cotton boundary response and discuss the implications for holographic reconstruction.

Abstract: The tree-level open superstring has an integer mass-squared spectrum on linear Regge trajectories. To what extent is this fixed by consistency conditions: locality, analyticity, crossing, unitarity together with the requirement that $2\to2$ scattering minimize the entanglement it generates? Using the local two-channel crossing symmetric dispersion relation with one-parameter ambiguity ($λ$-CSDR) we set up a bootstrap treating both the mass-squared spectrum and the partial-wave coefficients as unknowns which lets the machine learning algorithm to learn the mass-squared spectrum and partial-wave coefficients simultaneously, going beyond earlier work in which the spectrum was fixed. Using the local three-channel crossing symmetric dispersion relation with one-parameter ambiguity we obtain, within the class of amplitudes with soft Regge behavior and infinite higher-spin tower, a bound on the leading Wilson coefficients that we compare with existing bounds and trace to spin-0 dominance. We locate the Virasoro-Shapiro amplitude in the allowed region of the Wilson coefficients space. For amplitudes with two-channel crossing symmetry we find that a Non-dual amplitude exhibits a Polchinski-Strassler type transition from Regge to power-law behavior.

Abstract: The phase structure of the charged Gauss-Bonnet AdS black holes varies with the spacetime dimension. Within an appropriate parameter range, only the six-dimensional case admits two physical critical points and it is an ideal model for investigating supercritical thermodynamics in black hole systems with multiple critical points. Abstract: Monodromy line defects are of interest in the defect bootstrap program and are dual to electrically charged objects in dual gauge field descriptions. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非最小耦合幺模暴胀模型在爱因斯坦帧下的可接受性，特别是检验其是否能同时满足宇宙微波背景观测约束（如ns、r）与量子引力启发的Swampland猜想（如de Sitter猜想、弱引力猜想）。

Method: 采用规范场映射，在爱因斯坦帧中直接处理幺模暴胀模型；解析推导慢滚参数、e-fold数关系、结束条件及标量谱振幅归一化；对三种Swampland判据（FRDSSC、SWGC、SSWGC）进行解析极限分析与数值扫描验证。

Result: 模型存在内在张力：ε_V=1的暴胀结束要求ξ>1/2，而近原点慢滚要求ξ≪1且给出ns≈1−8ξ；SWGC在驻点对任意非零ξ均被违反，SSWGC要求ξ≳0.0643；大场极限下两弱引力判据统一要求ξ≥0.1；ACT/P-ACT观测区间不满足SWGC或SSWGC，仅FRDSSC可在a≈1时满足。

Conclusion: 该幺模暴胀模型虽可在孤立场值处拟合(ns,r)观测值，但无法支撑完整的50–60 e-fold暴胀历史，亦不能同时满足全部三个Swampland判据，表明其处于Swampland边缘或内部。

Abstract: We study non-minimal unimodular inflation in the Einstein frame; the effective potential takes the form $U(\varphi)=U_0(1+ξ\varphi^2/\mpl^2)^{-2}$. We consider the canonical field map and the inflationary observables directly from the Einstein-frame action. The exact potential slow-roll parameters, the leading scalar running, the scalar-amplitude normalization, the e-fold relation, and the end-of-inflation condition are obtained analytically. The model has a nontrivial structural tension: an $ε_V=1$ exit requires $ξ>1/2$, whereas the near-origin slow-roll regime requires $ξ\ll1$ and gives $n_s\simeq1-8ξ$. We next test three distinct swampland diagnostics. The further-refined de Sitter conjecture (FRDSSC) introduces the independent conjectural parameters $a$, $b$, and $q$, while the scalar weak gravity conjecture (SWGC) and its strong form (SSWGC) contain no analogous tunable conjecture parameter in the single-field realization adopted here. All three criteria are evaluated using the canonically normalized Einstein-frame scalar. Analytic limits show that the SWGC is violated at the stationary point for every nonzero $ξ$, whereas the SSWGC at that point requires $ξ\gtrsim0.0643$. In the large-field limit both scalar weak-gravity diagnostics reduce to the same threshold $ξ\ge0.1$. Numerical scans then show that the observationally relevant ACT/P-ACT region does not satisfy either scalar weak-gravity criterion, while the FRDSSC can be satisfied for $a$ close to unity. Consequently, the minimal model can reproduce acceptable $(n_s,r)$ at isolated field values, but this does not constitute a complete 50-60 e-fold inflationary history or simultaneous satisfaction of all three conjectures.

</details>


### [103] [Reconstructing the Goldberg-Palatini Action from Noether Identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28065)
*F. T. Brandt,Augusto C. B. Carvalho*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过微分同胚不变性的诺特定律重构一阶引力作用量，利用对称扰动场和迹调整连接作为独立场，在不依赖能量-动量张量选择的前提下，唯一确定了Goldberg-Palatini作用量及完整的二阶引力作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免传统自耦合方法中因能量-动量张量选择带来的不确定性，从对称性原理出发直接重构引力作用量。

Method: 基于微分同胚不变性导出的诺特定律，结合尺度变换约束、洛伦兹协变试探作用量展开、张量基展开与线性方程组求解。

Result: 证明在d>2维中作用量至多为三次，唯一可能的三次项为φGG顶点；通过诺特定律确定31个系数（d≥4），唯一恢复Goldberg-Palatini作用量，并精确消去连接场，得到完整二阶引力作用量及其无穷多引力子顶点。

Conclusion: 仅凭微分同胚不变性与基本维度分析即可唯一确定经典引力作用量形式，无需额外物理假设或人为张量定义，凸显对称性在理论构建中的核心地位。

Abstract: We investigate the reconstruction of the first-order gravitational action from the Noether identities associated with diffeomorphism invariance. The independent fields are a symmetric perturbation $φ^{μν}$ of the contravariant metric density and a trace-adjusted connection $G^λ{}_{μν}$, whose transformation laws are prescribed. For a local action polynomial in these fields, with the gravitational coupling as its only dimensionful constant and each additional field carrying one power of it, a dilatation, which is a particular diffeomorphism, shows that in any dimension $d>2$ the action terminates at most at cubic order, its only possible cubic term being a derivative-free $φGG$ vertex. Expanding the kernels of the resulting Lorentz-covariant ansatz in tensor bases gives 31 coefficients for $d\geq 4$. The Noether identities with general diffeomorphisms, expanded order by order in the fields, yield a linear system that eliminates the $φφ$ kernel and determines the remaining coefficients up to an overall normalization, recovering the Goldberg-Palatini action. Since the connection can be eliminated exactly, this also fixes the complete second-order action, with its infinite set of graviton vertices. The construction requires no choice of energy-momentum tensor, the source of ambiguity in the self-coupling approach. The kernel bases, the identities and the solution are given in appendices, and the symbolic computations are publicly available.

</details>


### [104] [Possible Microscopic source of torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28125)
*A. Capolupo,S. Monda,G. Pisacane,A. Quaranta,R. Serao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究轴向费米子相互作用与挠率背景之间的联系，通过四次自旋-自旋相互作用的平均场近似，导出费米子哈密顿量，并分析其粒子/反粒子凝聚及轴向电流的自发产生；进一步探讨挠率对中微子振荡、凝聚密度和CP不对称性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索真空凝聚与挠率背景之间的微观联系，特别是轴向费米子相互作用能否为轴向挠率提供微观起源。

Method: 采用四次自旋-自旋相互作用的平均场约化，得到二次费米子哈密顿量；通过对其对角化定义粒子与反粒子凝聚，并由自洽条件判断真空是否产生非零轴向电流；结合截断、涨落与能量条件评估近似有效性；最后在挠率背景下分析混合狄拉克场（特别是中微子）的动力学。

Result: 发现轴向期望值可作为轴向挠率的微观源；在中微子体系中，挠率导致味Bogoliubov系数、自旋依赖振荡频率、凝聚密度及CP不对称性的修正。

Conclusion: 轴向费米子相互作用可在特定条件下诱导非零轴向真空流，从而为挠率背景提供微观机制；该机制显著影响中微子物理行为，尤其在忽略曲率、仅考虑挠率时。

Abstract: Axial fermionic interactions connect vacuum condensation with torsionful back- grounds. We consider a quartic spin spin interaction whose mean field reduction yields a quadratic fermionic Hamiltonian. Its diagonalisation defines a particle and antiparticle condensate, while self consistency determines whether the vacuum devel- ops a non zero axial current. Cutoff, fluctuation and energy conditions delimit the validity of the approximation. When torsion couples to spin, this axial expectation value can provide a microscopic source for an axial torsion background. We then consider mixed Dirac fields on such a background. In the explicit neutrino analysis, metric curvature is neglected while torsion modifies the flavour Bogoliubov coeffi- cients, producing spin dependent oscillation frequencies, condensate densities and CP asymmetries.

</details>


### [105] [Surface defects on lens space and transfer matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28165)
*Kazunobu Maruyoshi,Toshihiro Ota,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维N=2和N=1类S理论在L(r,1)×S¹上的表面缺陷，通过差分算子刻画其对透镜空间指标的影响，并揭示其与Sklyanin代数及自旋链转移矩阵的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解四维超对称规范理论（特别是类S及其N=1推广类S_k）中表面缺陷对透镜空间上指标的影响，以及这些缺陷如何由代数结构（如Sklyanin代数）和可积系统（如自旋链）统一描述。

Method: 利用透镜空间指标的TQFT结构（源于S-对偶性），构造并分析作用于味对称性荷和全纯性的差分算子；对A₁型理论显式求出基本缺陷对应的闭式差分算子；将算子表示为L-算子的迹，进而关联到Sklyanin代数；对类S_k理论提出并验证缺陷算子即为基于该L-算子的非均匀自旋链转移矩阵。

Result: 得到A₁型理论中基本缺陷（pʳ、qʳ、pq型及r为奇数时的半整数平移型）的显式差分算子；证明所有算子相互对易且由基本算子生成；发现这些算子对应于以qʳ为nome、pq为shift的Sklyanin代数的迹；指出p-型与q-型代数构成透镜空间版本的椭圆模双代数；提出并验证类S_k理论的缺陷算子即为其对应自旋链的转移矩阵。

Conclusion: 表面缺陷在透镜空间上的效应可系统地由可积系统语言（L-算子、Sklyanin代数、转移矩阵）精确刻画，揭示了超对称场论、几何拓扑（透镜空间）、代数结构与量子可积性之间的深层统一。

Abstract: We study surface defects in four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ theories of class $\mathcal{S}$ and $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories of class $\mathcal{S}_k$ placed on $L(r,1)\times S^1$. The lens space index depends on the fugacities $p$ and $q$, and the effect of the defects on it is captured by difference operators acting on the fugacities and holonomies of flavor symmetries. For theories of type $A_1$ we determine in closed form the difference operators for the basic defects, labeled by $p^r$, $q^r$ and $pq$, and show that for odd $r$ there exist additional elementary defects, whose operators shift the holonomy by one half modulo $r$. All the operators commute, and the elementary operators generate the others. For even $r$ the same holds once the holonomy is extended to a twisted sector. We show that the elementary operators are traces of $L$-operators whose entries generate a Sklyanin algebra, with nome $q^r$ and shift $pq$. The lens space enters only through the parameters of the algebra and through its representation, which is realized on a doubled holonomy label. The $p$- and $q$-type algebras form a lens space version of the elliptic modular double. For class $\mathcal{S}_k$ theories we propose, and test in several ways, that the defect operators are transfer matrices of inhomogeneous spin chains built from these $L$-operators. Throughout, we use the TQFT structure of the lens space index, which follows from S-duality, both to fix and to check the operators.

</details>


### [106] [Finite Spectral Recurrences for Loop Integrals in de Sitter and Anti-de Sitter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28183)
*Bruce A. Bassett,Sergio Cacciatori,Ugo Moschella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于de Sitter和anti-de Sitter空间中圈图积分的新方法，类比平直空间中的分部积分恒等式，通过一种通用的局域、坐标无关的传播子代数导出谱参数的精确有限差分方程，并应用于两圈水melon图和一圈二点、四点函数的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空（如dS和AdS）中缺乏类似平直空间中强大的积分技巧（如IBP），限制了圈图计算的发展；需要建立一套适用于弯曲时空的系统性代数方法。

Method: 构建适用于dS和AdS的局域、坐标无关传播子代数，由此导出关于谱参数的有限差分方程；引入外部点后推广为微分-差分方程；在AdS边界极限下验证其与已知Witten图关系的一致性。

Result: 成功求解两圈watermelon图并分离出紫外极点；给出了一圈二点和四点函数的解析结果；方程具有普适性，原则上适用于任意费曼图。

Conclusion: 该方法为dS/AdS圈图计算提供了统一、代数化的新框架，弥合了弯曲时空与平直空间高阶微扰计算方法之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: We introduce a method that does for loop integrals in de Sitter and anti-de Sitter space what integration-by-parts identities do in flat space. A local, coordinate-free propagator algebra common to both geometries yields exact finite-difference equations in spectral labels; with external points they become differential-difference equations, and at the AdS boundary recover known Witten-diagram relations. Acting line by line, they apply in principle to any diagram. We solve them for the two-loop watermelon, isolating its ultraviolet poles, and for one-loop two- and four-point functions.

</details>


### [107] [Causality violation in the holographic approach to N=4 SYM theory at finite 't Hooft coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28319)
*Alex Buchel,Pavel Kovtun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出，在AdS/CFT对应中，使用截断至(α')³阶的超引力方程来计算N=4 SYM理论中能量动量张量关联函数，会导致热平衡态在任意大的有限‘t Hooft耦合下出现动力学不稳定性，根源在于将微扰导出的方程误当作非微扰有效方程，造成因果律破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在有限但大't Hooft耦合下，用高阶α'修正的超引力计算N=4 SYM关联函数时，常用全息方案是否可靠，尤其关注其是否隐含物理上不可接受的后果（如因果违反与不稳定性）。

Method: 分析截断至(α')³阶的超引力运动方程所对应的全息关联函数计算方案，类比五维Gauss-Bonnet引力中AdS黑洞膜的不稳定性机制，考察其对N=4 SYM热平衡态动力学稳定性的影响。

Result: 发现该截断方案导致N=4 SYM平面极限下的热平衡态在任意大的有限't Hooft耦合下均动力学不稳定；该不稳定性是因果律被破坏的体现，源于将微扰方程非微扰使用。

Conclusion: 任何O(α'³)全息方案的结果仅在严格微扰意义下有效；不能外推至强耦合非微扰区域；需谨慎对待高阶α'修正的全息计算及其物理解释。

Abstract: In the AdS/CFT correspondence, correlation functions of the energy-momentum tensor in N=4 supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory at large but finite 't Hooft coupling can be studied using higher-derivative corrections to supergravity. We show that the commonly used holographic prescription for correlation functions which is obtained by truncating the equations of motion at order $(α')^3$ implies that the thermal equilibrium state of N=4 SYM theory in the planar limit is dynamically unstable at arbitrarily large finite 't Hooft coupling. The instability is a reflection of the unphysical causality violation brought about by treating perturbatively-derived equations as if they were non-perturbative, analogous to the instability of AdS black branes in five-dimensional Gauss-Bonnet gravity. While other holographic prescriptions for treating the $O(α'^3)$ corrections to supergravity may alleviate the pathology introduced by the truncation, the validity regime of the results obtained by any prescription at $O(α'^3)$ must be limited to the strict perturbative treatment of the $O(α'^3)$ effects on N=4 SYM observables.

</details>


### [108] [Cosmological correlators from the Euclidean sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28516)
*Andrei Katsevich,Zimo Sun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过欧氏球面解析延拓计算德西特时空晚期宇宙关联子的新框架，将任意质量标量场的关联子统一表示为广义欧拉积分，并验证了其与Schwinger-Keldysh形式等价，同时利用Landau分析确定动量空间奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一种系统、普适且便于解析分析的计算晚期宇宙关联子的方法，以克服传统in-in形式在解析结构研究中的困难。

Method: 基于欧氏球面到洛伦兹型德西特时空的解析延拓，将宇宙关联子表达为广义欧拉积分；结合Landau分析研究其动量空间奇点结构；并与Schwinger-Keldysh形式下的树图和圈图结果比对验证。

Result: 成功将各类质量标量场的晚期关联子统一表示为广义欧拉积分；复现了Schwinger-Keldysh形式下多类图的结果，验证了两种形式等价；明确了关联子动量空间奇点由Landau条件决定。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究宇宙关联子的解析结构提供了系统、普适且强有力的工具，强化了欧氏方法在宇宙学微扰计算中的基础地位。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for computing late-time cosmological correlators in de Sitter spacetime by analytic continuation from the Euclidean sphere. In this framework, correlators of arbitrary massive scalar fields take the universal form of generalized Euler integrals. Using this representation, we reproduce the results obtained using the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism for a range of tree-level and loop diagrams, providing nontrivial checks of the equivalence between the Euclidean and the in-in formalisms. We further show that a Landau analysis of the generalized Euler integrals determines the singularities of the corresponding correlators in momentum space. This framework provides a systematic approach to studying the analytic structure of cosmological correlators.

</details>


### [109] [Towards differential expansion for generic representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28531)
*Ya. Kononov,A. Morozov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于显式计算的彩色HOMFLY-PT多项式，研究了微分展开（DE）结构，重点解决非矩形表示下的DE基底构造与缺陷特征改进问题，提出以扭结、双辫和3-pretzel等演化族通过三角变换推导DE，并发现缺陷应由非递减序列{k_j}而非单一数δ^K刻画，且旧假设δ+1=degΔ在部分非交替扭结上失效；同时验证了非矩形表示下HOMFLY多项式对变种扭结对的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微分展开（DE）在对称与矩形表示中已有较好理解，但在非矩形表示R下，其扭结无关的基底Z_R^X尚未明确；此外，原有缺陷δ^K的单参数刻画被认为过于简化，需更精细的特征描述。

Method: 利用扭结、双辫和3-pretzel等演化族，通过特殊的三角变换处理演化系数以推导DE；引入非递减序列{k_j}重新刻画DE缺陷，并结合Alexander多项式Δ(q²)及Melvin-Morton-Rozansky-Kashaev极限的推广形式分析上下界；同时对比多组变种扭结对的非矩形彩色HOMFLY多项式差异。

Result: 成功为若干演化族构造出Z_R^X基底并验证其跨扭结的近似普适性；发现DE缺陷应由序列{k_j}描述，其受两个阶梯函数约束；旧关系δ+1=degΔ对19个11–12交叉非交替扭结不成立；非矩形彩色HOMFLY在变种扭结对间表现出高度一致性，支持DE精神。

Conclusion: DE结构比以往认知更丰富，缺陷需用序列而非单参数刻画；Z_R^X具有强普适性但需考虑缺陷导致的可控约化；非矩形表示下的HOMFLY多项式展现出良好的扭结无关性，为统一理解彩色不变量提供了新路径。

Abstract: We report the first results of phenomenological analysis, based on extended set of explicitly calculated colored HOMFLY-PT polynomials in \cite{knotebook}. They concern differential expansion (DE), which separates the dependencies on the knot ${\cal K}$ and the representation $R$. %of colored HOMFLY-PT polynomials. DE is well understood for symmetric and rectangular representations, while for non-rectangular $R$ even the knot-independent basis $Z_R^X$ of DE is not known. We concentrate on two issues: the possible derivation of DE and on improved characteristic of its defect. We discuss how evolution families -- twist knots, double braids and 3-pretzels -- can be used to find DE by a peculiar triangular transformation of evolution coefficients. Once $Z^R_X$ are found for particular family, they are expected to stay basically the same for arbitrary knots -- modulo controllable reductions, characterized by the defect of DE, which puts knots into different universality classes. Contrary to original expectation, these classes appear labeled not by a single number $δ^{\cal K}$, but by a whole non-decreasing sequence $\{k_j\}$, which is squeezed between two staircases, ${\rm entier}\left(\frac{j-1}{δ+1}\right)\leq k_j\leq{\rm entier}\left(\frac{j-1}{{\rm deg}\,Δ}\right)$, where the upper bound involves Alexander polynomial $Δ(q^2)$ and follows from the continuation of the Melvin--Morton--Rozansky-Kashaev limit to $A=q^{-m}$ with $m>0$, when the limit gives a {\it positive} power of Alexander. The old assumption $δ+1={\rm deg}\,Δ$ is oversimplified and fails for 19 non-alternating knots with 11 and 12 crossings. Last, we list the differences of non-rectangularly colored HOMFLY for various mutant pairs to demonstrate their universality (knot-independence) and nice consistency with the spirit of DE.

</details>


### [110] [Energy-Energy Correlators at Strong Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21139)
*Max Jackson,Lecheng Ren,Bo Wang,Congkao Wen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在强耦合极限下，N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中的能量-能量关联器（EEC），通过世界面表示和AdS Virasoro-Shapiro振幅，计算了不同维度半BPS态下的EEC展开至λ^{-3/2}阶，并对p=2情形完成了至λ^{-2}阶的二阶曲率修正；同时提出并验证了一种基于低能展开Wilson系数的互补方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强耦合下规范场论中事件形状可观测量（如EEC）的行为，并与非微扰约束进行比较以检验理论一致性。

Method: 利用AdS/CFT对应，从世界面表示的AdS Virasoro-Shapiro振幅出发，处理带Kaluza-Klein外线的半BPS态；同时发展基于低能展开中Wilson系数提取EEC强耦合系数的互补方法。

Result: 得到任意维p半BPS态下EEC至λ^{-3/2}阶的解析结果；对p=2完成至λ^{-2}阶的二阶曲率修正；两种方法结果完全一致，且新修正改善了与中间耦合区非微扰界的一致性。

Conclusion: 强耦合EEC可通过世界面振幅系统计算，所获高阶修正增强理论预测可靠性；两种独立方法的一致性佐证了AdS/CFT框架下事件形状计算的稳健性。

Abstract: We study energy-energy correlators (EEC) in planar $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory at strong 't Hooft coupling $λ$. We consider the EEC in states created by half-BPS operators of arbitrary dimension $p$, and determine the corresponding event-shape function up to order $λ^{-3/2}$ from the worldsheet representation of the AdS Virasoro-Shapiro amplitude with Kaluza-Klein external states. For $p=2$ we compute the second curvature correction, which completes the EEC through order $λ^{-2}$; the new contribution improves the agreement with recently derived non-perturbative bounds at intermediate coupling. We further develop a complementary method in which the strong-coupling expansion coefficients of the EEC are extracted directly from the Wilson coefficients of low-energy expansion of the AdS Virasoro-Shapiro amplitude, and find the two approaches in perfect agreement.

</details>

*Alessandro Georgoudis,Max Jackson,Congkao Wen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中重态的能量-能量关联器（EEC），在大电荷't Hooft极限下，通过四点相关函数的双不连续性提取EEC，并分析其在有限有效耦合下的物理行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强耦合规范理论中能量流动的结构，特别是在大R电荷极限下EEC的行为。

Method: 利用大电荷't Hooft极限下已知的重-重-轻-轻四点相关函数（对有效耦合λ_Q全阶已知），通过提取其双不连续性来确定EEC。

Conclusion: EEC在大电荷极限下可被精确计算，揭示了耦合强度与角度依赖之间的非平凡关系，为强耦合动力学提供了新见解。

Abstract: We study the energy-energy correlator (EEC) in heavy states of four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory in the large-charge 't Hooft limit, in which the dimension $Δ$ of the heavy operator (or, equivalently, its $R$-charge) is taken to infinity with the effective coupling $λ_Q \propto g_{\mathrm{YM}}^2Δ$ held fixed. The relevant heavy-heavy-light-light four-point correlators are known to all orders in $λ_Q$ in this limit. For certain classes of operators, the perturbative series can be resummed to obtain exact finite-coupling expressions. We use these results to determine the corresponding EEC, whose dynamical part can be extracted from a double discontinuity of the four-point correlator. We determine the EEC at finite effective coupling and study its physical behavior as a function of $λ_Q$ and the angular separation between the detectors, both at generic angles and in the collinear and back-to-back limits.

</details>

### [112] [Minimal-length kinematics of coherent light and a proposed two-color vacuum chronoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26892)
