TL;DR: 本文提出了一种避免‘足球球问题’的量子引力效应检验新方法：通过在量子场论框架下将广义不确定性原理（GUP）作用于单模而非整体动量，并证明宏观相干光脉冲的群速度仅取决于中心波长处的单光子GUP修正，与总能量无关；进而设计基于双色真空计时的实验方案，利用飞秒拍瓦激光及其高次谐波，在地面实验室中以可控方式探测普朗克尺度运动学效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一量子力学与广义相对论预期存在最小可测长度，从而引出广义不确定性原理（GUP）和能量依赖的真空群速度；但传统将GUP应用于复合系统质心动量会导致被观测排除的过大速度偏差（即‘足球球问题’），亟需一种物理自洽且可观测的替代方案。

Method: 采用量子场论自然的单模GUP修正方案（修改单模对易关系），显式计算多模相干光脉冲的宏观群速度；推导其与单光子GUP速度及经典高斯光束因子的关系；构建双色真空计时实验模型，通过调控光束束腰分离普朗克尺度运动学延迟与经典衍射延迟，并定量评估残余气体色散与谐波产生时间偏移等主导系统误差。

Result: 证实宏观相干光脉冲群速度严格还原为单光子GUP速度乘以经典高斯因子，不依赖总能量，彻底规避‘足球球问题’；飞秒拍瓦脉冲与单个高能光子探测同一GUP参数空间；所提双色实验方案在束腰扫描下可实现对Planck尺度速度修正的源无关、可控地面探测，灵敏度可达当前天体物理约束量级。

Conclusion: GUP应作用于场的单模而非宏观质心动量，该原则使宏观激光脉冲成为探测量子引力运动学效应的理想探针；提出的双色真空计时实验为地面实验室检验普朗克尺度物理提供了切实可行、系统误差可控的新范式。

Abstract: The unification of quantum theory and general relativity suggests the existence of a minimal measurable length, leading to a generalized uncertainty principle (GUP) and an energy-dependent vacuum group velocity. Testing these effects with macroscopic objects typically encounters the ``soccer-ball problem,'' where applying Planck-scale deformations to the total momentum predicts observationally ruled-out velocity deviations. We adopt the QFT-natural prescription in which the GUP modifies single-mode commutators, and verify by explicit calculation that the macroscopic group velocity of a multimode coherent pulse reduces to the single-photon GUP velocity evaluated at the central wavelength, multiplied by the classical Gaussian-beam factor, with no dependence on the total pulse energy. The energy--momentum relation $E=pc$ itself is unmodified; the deformation enters through the velocity--momentum relation and is equivalent to a modified dispersion relation in the wave vector, with a quadratic coefficient that depends on which of the two variables is used. The soccer-ball problem $\,$---$\,$ which arises only when the GUP is applied to the center-of-mass momentum of a composite system $\,$---$\,$ is therefore absent for coherent light, and macroscopic petawatt pulses probe the same parameter space as single high-energy photons. Building on this framework, we propose a two-color vacuum chronoscopy experiment using a short-pulse petawatt laser and its high-harmonic generation. By systematically varying the macroscopic beam waist, our setup separates Planck-scale kinematic shifts from classical diffraction delays, establishing a controlled, source-independent terrestrial methodology whose sensitivity we assess against current astrophysical bounds and against the dominant waist-independent systematics that limit it, residual-gas dispersion and the harmonic birth-time offset.

</details>


### [113] [Elementary-Symmetric Ricci Gravity: Metric Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26980)
*Xingpao Suo,Dianxu Ma,Yuqiong Shan,Xingyan Suo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种四维度规引力理论，其拉格朗日量是里奇张量初等对称多项式的线性组合；推导了场方程，分析了爱因斯坦真空解和平行里奇真空解的性质，并研究了其线性稳定性与宇宙学演化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基于里奇张量对称多项式构造的新型四维引力理论，以理解其真空结构、稳定性及宇宙学含义（如暴胀终结与加速膨胀）。

Method: 构建以里奇张量初等对称多项式为基本变量的广义引力作用量；变分推导场方程；分类求解爱因斯坦真空与可对角化平行里奇真空解；在线性化框架下分离标量与自旋-2自由度并分析其稳定性；推导广义Friedmann约束与均匀演化方程。

Result: 发现平行里奇真空包含两类常曲率二维曲面的直积解，可呈现曲率半径层级；爱因斯坦真空存在多支解，对应多项式不同实根，多重根界定参数空间边界；线性化分析表明存在不稳定的初始dS解向稳定dS解演化的典型空间平坦宇宙学解。

Conclusion: 该理论提供了无需引入标量场即可实现暴胀启动与自然终止的纯几何引力机制，有望为早期与晚期宇宙加速膨胀提供统一解释。

Abstract: We investigate a four-dimensional metric theory of gravity whose Lagrangian is a linear combination of the elementary symmetric polynomials of the Ricci tensor. We derive the field equations and characterize Einstein vacua and a real, diagonalizable class of parallel-Ricci vacua. The latter includes direct products of two constant-curvature surfaces and can exhibit a hierarchy of curvature radii. The Einstein vacua possess multiple branches associated with the distinct roots of a polynomial, whose multiple roots define boundaries in parameter space. We derive the linearized equations on maximally symmetric backgrounds, distinguish the scalar and spin-2 degrees of freedom, and then analyze their classical linear stability. We also obtain the generalized Friedmann constraint and the associated homogeneous evolution equations. An illustrative spatially flat solution evolves from the neighborhood of an unstable de Sitter solution toward a stable de Sitter solution. This behavior may motivate further investigation into early- and late-time accelerated expansion and provide a gravity-driven mechanism for automatic end of inflation.

</details>


### [114] [Rotating Barrow entropy-inspired wormholes versus black holes: null trajectories, shadow morphology, and exotic matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26981)
*A. Errehymy,G. Yergaliyeva,S. K. Maurya,B. Turimov,I. Ibragimov,J. Rayimbaev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个由熵启发的正则物质分布支撑的旋转可穿越虫洞，研究了其几何结构、类光测地线、阴影形态及奇异物质含量。通过Barrow变形的Bekenstein-Hawking面积律导出几何，引入慢速旋转以保持无视界、可穿越性；发现光子球位置与自转方向相关，阴影呈现不对称性；能量条件被违反，但奇异物质局域化受Barrow指数调控；阴影直径存在简并性，而光子环结构和旋转不对称性可作为区分虫洞与黑洞的关键观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基于熵修正（Barrow变形）的正则物质能否支持物理上更合理的旋转可穿越虫洞，并寻找可观测的、区别于黑洞的独特引力特征（如阴影不对称性、光子环结构）。

Method: 基于Barrow变形的熵面积律推导虫洞度规；引入慢旋转近似（N(r,θ)和ω(r)）；求解赤道面类光测地线得到光子球与临界冲击参数；分析能量条件违背程度与体积积分量IV；对比Kerr黑洞与正则黑洞的阴影特征。

Result: 获得一个无奇点、无视界、可穿越的旋转虫洞解；能量密度ρ_B∝−r^{−n−3}处处为负，满足喉部张开条件；光子球位置随自转分裂（r_ph^2≈6J对进动分支）；阴影呈约10%的分数不对称性（𝒜≈0.10）；弱/零能量条件被违反，且违反强度由Barrow指数n调控；体积积分量IV在特定条件下有限。

Conclusion: 熵启发的Barrow变形能自然产生正则奇异物质分布，支撑物理上更自洽的旋转虫洞；其阴影的旋转不对称性和光子环结构可作为关键观测判据，打破与黑洞（尤其是Kerr和正则黑洞）在阴影直径上的简并性，为未来EHT等观测提供新识别途径。

Abstract: We construct a rotating traversable wormhole supported by a regular, entropy-inspired matter distribution and investigate its geometry, null geodesics, shadow morphology, and exotic matter content. We derive the geometry from the Barrow deformation of the Bekenstein--Hawking area law, characterized by an exponent $0<n\le1$, which replaces point-like divergences with a regular matter profile. The resulting energy density is negative everywhere and scales as $ρ_B\propto-r^{-n-3}$, while the shape function smoothly satisfies the flare-out condition at the throat. We introduce slow rotation via $N(r,θ)=\exp[αJ^2\cos^2θ/r^2]$ and $ω(r)=2J/r^3$; both remain finite at the throat, ensuring a horizon-free, traversable geometry. Equatorial null geodesics yield a photon-sphere condition distinct from the standard black-hole relation. In the static limit, the photon sphere sits at the throat, $r_{\rm ph}=r_{\rm th}$; for slow rotation, the prograde branch moves to $r_{\rm ph}^2\simeq 6J$, while the retrograde branch is pushed toward the throat. The critical impact parameters are $\mathscr{B}_{\pm}\approx\pm r_{\rm ph}+2J/r_{\rm ph}$. For $M=1$, $r_{\rm th}=1$, $J/M^2=0.5$, this gives $r_{\rm ph}\approx1.732$, $\mathscr{B}_{+}\approx1.90$, $\mathscr{B}_{-}\approx-1.55$, and a fractional asymmetry $\mathcal{A}\approx0.10$. The weak and null energy conditions are violated, with the strongest violation near the throat, while the Barrow exponent controls the localization of exotic matter. The volume-integral quantifier $I_V$ remains finite when $r_{\rm th}^{n+1}=4M/[(n+2)π^{n/2}]$. Comparisons with Kerr and regular black holes indicate that shadow diameter alone may be degenerate, whereas photon-ring structure and rotational asymmetry provide complementary signatures capable of distinguishing this entropy-inspired wormhole from black-hole alternatives.

</details>


### [115] [Gravitational baryogenesis in asymptotically safe gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27020)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Gravitational baryogenesis in asymptotically safe gravity is considered. Two baryogenesis interactions are explored and their effects on the baryon-to-entropy ratio are discussed during the radiation era. The model's parameters are constrained with the current observational data, indicating these constructions may provide viable explanations for the observed baryon asymmetry.

</details>


### [116] [Nonlinear Response of Schwarzschild Black Holes at Arbitrary Perturbative Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27048)
*Jiaqi Chen,Yu-Cun Xie*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了递归分部积分恒等式，将Regge-Wheeler-Zerilli方程的非齐次解符号化约化为少量主积分，并推导出用于快速、准确、稳定数值计算这些主积分的闭合常微分方程组；以此为基础，计算了二次、三次和四次拟正则模耦合系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为高效、精确地计算施瓦西黑洞在任意微扰阶数下的非线性响应核，需解决Regge-Wheeler-Zerilli方程非齐次解的复杂积分问题。

Method: 建立递归分部积分恒等式以符号化约化非齐次解至主积分，并推导闭合常微分方程系统实现主积分的快速稳定数值求解。

Result: 获得任意阶非线性响应核的解析结构；成功计算出二次、三次和四次拟正则模耦合系数。

Conclusion: 该方法为研究黑洞微扰理论中的高阶非线性效应提供了系统、高效且数值稳定的工具。

Abstract: We establish recursive integration-by-parts identities that symbolically reduce the inhomogeneous solutions of the Regge-Wheeler-Zerilli equation to a small set of master integrals, yielding the nonlinear response kernels of Schwarzschild black holes at arbitrary perturbative order. We further derive a closed ODE system that enables rapid, accurate, and stable numerical evaluation of these master integrals. To demonstrate our result, we compute quadratic, cubic, and quartic quasinormal-mode coupling coefficients as special cases of the response kernels.

</details>


### [117] [Deglobalising the fit: how to search for physics beyond vacuum General Relativity in LISA catalogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27069)
*Francisco Duque,Jonathan Gair*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对LISA（激光干涉空间天线）数据中大量重叠引力波信号的后处理分析策略，指出传统两步法会因‘掩蔽效应’导致对广义相对论偏离信号的检测灵敏度下降，并引入‘掩蔽比’R量化该损失；作者给出从真空全局拟合后验样本计算R的方法及优化再分析源选择的算法，并通过21个重叠啁啾正弦波的玩具模型验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA将观测数百万重叠引力波信号，对每个物理模型（如偏离真空广义相对论的模型）都进行全局拟合计算成本过高；现有两步后处理策略存在目标源的非真空效应被其他 nuisance 源吸收而无法在再分析中恢复的问题，亟需量化并缓解该偏差。

Method: 在线性信号近似下，定义掩蔽比R∈[0,1]刻画效应吸收程度；提出基于真空全局拟合后验样本计算R的方法；设计算法按各nuisance源与目标源的相关性排序，仅对最相关的若干源与目标联合再拟合（降尺度再分析）；通过含21个重叠啁啾正弦波（其中1个含微小非线性啁啾）的玩具模型，结合完整MCMC推断进行验证。

Result: 证明了掩蔽效应会导致信噪比衰减为R×nσ，零检验给出的限制也会被同等程度收紧；成功从后验样本中估计出R；算法能准确识别并排序关键nuisance源；玩具模型验证表明该方法可有效恢复被掩蔽的物理效应。

Conclusion: 所提方法为在真实LISA模拟数据中开展广义相对论检验提供了可行基础，且可拓展用于分析不同引力波源间系统误差的影响；强调了在密集重叠信号场景中，忽略掩蔽效应将导致模型比较和参数检验出现系统性偏差。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna will observe up to $\sim 10^{7}$ overlapping signals, which have to be characterised simultaneously with a global fit. This is a challenge for model comparison since rerunning the global fit for every physical model of interest, including deviations from vacuum General Relativity, is computationally demanding. An alternative is a two-step post-processing strategy: a first global fit with vacuum models only, after which a selected group of target sources is reanalysed under a different model with all other sources subtracted. We show that in this strategy a beyond-vacuum effect in a target source can be partly absorbed by the overlapping nuisance sources in the first fit, subtracted with them, and never reach the reanalysis. In the linear-signal approximation, the loss is captured by a single number, the masking ratio $\mathcal{R}\in[0,1]$. A deviation the global fit would detect at $n_σ$ is reported by post-processing at $\mathcal{R}\,n_σ$, and bounds in null tests are also reported too tight by the same factor. We show how to compute $\mathcal{R}$ from the posterior samples of the vacuum global fit, and give an algorithm that ranks the nuisance sources by how much of the effect each can absorb, so that only those most correlated with it need to be refitted with the target in a down-scaled reanalysis with a few sources. We validate our method with full Markov-chain Monte Carlo inference on a toy model of 21 overlapping chirping sinusoids, where one carries a small non-linear chirp that represents the missed physics, while the other 20 act as nuisance sources. This method can serve as a basis for tests of General Relativity in realistic LISA mock datasets and can be adapted to study the impact of systematics between different gravitational-wave sources.

</details>


### [118] [Binary Black Hole Puncture Initial Data with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27094)
*Thiago Assumpção,Ignacio Brevis,Irene Pitsiladi,Martin Zinzen,Miguel Bezares*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pinndorama，一种用于求解穿刺形式下哈密顿约束的物理信息神经网络（PINN）代码。该方法仅依赖控制方程和边界条件进行训练，无需解析或微扰引导解，并在曲线坐标系中采样配点；采用Adam与自缩放Broyden拟牛顿法两阶段优化；在球对称、轴对称双穿刺及三维GW150914类配置上验证了高精度；与NRPyElliptic求解器相比，相对L2误差达10^{-8}（轴对称）和10^{-5}（三维）；并利用参数化PINN提供物理启发的初值，将NRPyElliptic超松弛时间缩短约4.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值相对论中椭圆型初值问题（如穿刺形式下的哈密顿约束）依赖迭代求解器，收敛慢且初值敏感；亟需一种不依赖先验解析解、能灵活处理复杂几何与参数变化的新型高效求解方法。

Method: 构建物理信息神经网络（PINN）Pinndorama，直接嵌入哈密顿约束方程与边界条件；在curvilinear坐标系中随机采样collocation点；采用两阶段优化：先用Adam快速粗收敛，再以自缩放Broyden拟牛顿法精调；扩展为参数化网络以支持不同穿刺自旋输入。

Result: 在球对称测试问题中复现精确解；轴对称双穿刺（不等自旋）和三维GW150914类配置（不等自旋+轨道角动量）中，与NRPyElliptic相比相对L2误差分别为~10^{-8}和~10^{-5}；参数化PINN提供的初值使NRPyElliptic超松弛时间降低约4.6倍。

Conclusion: PINNs可高精度求解二元穿刺初值问题，无需解析引导解，具备强泛化性与几何适应性；不仅能独立生成高质量初值，还可作为传统椭圆求解器的加速模块，为数值相对论提供新工具路径。

Abstract: We present Pinndorama, a physics-informed neural network (PINN) code for solving the Hamiltonian constraint in the puncture formalism. The networks are trained solely on the governing equation and boundary conditions, without an analytical or perturbative guiding solution, with collocation points sampled in curvilinear coordinates. Training combines two optimization algorithms: an initial stage using adaptive moment estimation (Adam), followed by the self-scaled Broyden quasi-Newton method. We apply this approach to a spherically symmetric test problem with a known exact solution, axisymmetric binary punctures with unequal spins, and a fully three-dimensional GW150914-like configuration with unequal spins and orbital momenta. Comparisons with the NRPyElliptic solver yield relative $L^2$ errors of order $10^{-8}$ in axisymmetry and $10^{-5}$ in three dimensions. In addition, we reduce the hyperbolic-relaxation time in NRPyElliptic by a factor of approximately $4.6$ by training a parametric network that takes the puncture spin as input and provides a physics-informed initial guess to the solver. These results demonstrate that PINNs can provide accurate binary-puncture initial data and complement conventional elliptic solvers in numerical relativity.

</details>


### [119] [Broken isospectrality in inverted black hole potentials and the emergence of Efimov scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27102)
*Umer Shahzad,Sebastian H. Völkel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Mashhoon边界态方法中倒置的Regge-Wheeler与Zerilli势是否等谱，首次直接计算了倒置Zerilli势的束缚态谱，发现其与倒置Regge-Wheeler势谱不同，并破坏了一阶Darboux关系；尽管如此，二者谱仍呈现类似Efimov物理的渐近几何标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究倒置Regge-Wheeler与Zerilli势在Mashhoon边界态方法中是否等谱，以检验其在黑洞准正规模计算中的理论一致性与适用性。

Method: 通过数值与解析方法扩展已有研究，首次直接计算倒置Zerilli势的束缚态谱；分析势反转对Darboux关系的影响；引入连续插值势并结合一阶与二阶微扰分析能级变化行为。

Result: 倒置Regge-Wheeler与Zerilli势具有不同的束缚态谱，且其一阶Darboux关系被破坏；能级偏移在势内部显著大于端点，需二阶微扰解释；两者谱均呈现Efimov型渐近几何标度律。

Conclusion: 倒置势虽不等谱，但仍共享普适渐近标度行为，这对基于束缚态高精度计算准正规模的新方法提出了理论警示与修正需求。

Abstract: Are the inverted Regge-Wheeler and Zerilli potentials arising in Mashhoon's bound-state approach to black-hole quasinormal modes isospectral? Extending previous numerical and analytical studies of the inverted Regge-Wheeler potential, we present the first direct computation of the bound-state spectrum of the inverted Zerilli potential. Although Chandrasekhar proved that the original potentials have identical quasinormal-mode spectra, we show that their inverted counterparts have distinct bound-state spectra. We also demonstrate that potential inversion destroys their first-order Darboux relation, used by Glampedakis et al. to provide a simpler proof of isospectrality. A continuous interpolation between the potentials reveals substantially larger energy shifts in the interior than at the endpoints, indicating cancellations that suppress the endpoint spectral splitting. Explaining this behavior requires second-order perturbative contributions, even towards the eikonal limit. Despite these differences, both spectra exhibit asymptotic geometric scaling characteristic of Efimov physics, first discovered in three-boson systems. This scaling also applies to other inverted potentials with the same leading tail, including the scalar-field case studied here. Beyond their theoretical implications, our results raise questions for recently developed methods that compute quasinormal modes with high precision from numerical bound states.

</details>


### [120] [Curvature Reconstruction and Tensor Polarizations in Isospectrality-Breaking Black-Hole Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27116)
*Pratik Wagle,Dongjun Li,Yanbei Chen,Nicolas Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了动态Chern-Simons引力理论中非旋转黑洞的环降相引力波，建立了曲率扰动、度规主变量与探测器张量极化之间的映射关系，发现标量场扰动不直接贡献于主导阶张量应变，为后续环降信号分析奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试广义相对论需将黑洞微扰动力学的修改与探测器应变中的可观测特征联系起来，特别是在动态Chern-Simons（dCS）引力这类修正引力理论中。

Method: 构建了扰动Weyl标量δΨ₀的偶宇称与奇宇称部分分别与Zerilli-Moncrief（ZM）和Regge-Wheeler（RW）主函数及标量径向扰动的映射；直接从度规扰动导出渐近张量极化h₊和hₓ；利用辐射型主函数重构应变。

Result: 偶宇称应变由ZM主函数主导，奇宇称由Cunningham-Price-Moncrief归一化的RW函数主导；标量径向扰动不产生独立的局域代数项贡献于主导阶张量应变；实现了曲率层面标量依赖性与张量通道标量频率响应的分离。

Conclusion: 该工作为在dCS引力中开展基于宇称分裂的拍频分析和有效环降频率提取提供了严格的解析重建基础，表明标量自由度对主导应变的影响是间接且高阶的。

Abstract: Testing general relativity with the ringdown phase of gravitational waves requires connecting modifications of black-hole perturbation dynamics to observable signatures in detector strain. In this work, we study the relation between curvature perturbations, metric master variables, and detector tensor polarizations for nonrotating black holes in dynamical Chern-Simons gravity. In this theory the axial gravitational perturbation couples to a dynamical scalar field, while the polar sector remains GR-like at the order considered. We first construct a map for the perturbed Weyl scalar $δΨ_0$, expressing its even-parity part in terms of the ZM master function and its odd-parity part in terms of the RW master function together with the scalar radial perturbation. We also derive the leading asymptotic tensor polarizations $h_+$ and $h_\times$ directly from the metric perturbations. The resulting strain is reconstructed from the radiative gravitational master functions: the ZM function in the even sector and the Cunningham-Price-Moncrief-normalized odd function in the axial sector for dynamical Chern-Simons gravity. No independent local algebraic term proportional to the scalar radial function appears in the leading tensor strain. These results separate curvature-level scalar dependence from tensor-channel scalar-frequency responses and provide the formal reconstruction basis for companion work on parity-split beating and effective ringdown frequencies.

</details>


### [121] [Model and parameter-path dependence of charged Dirac quasi-bound spectra in Born--Infeld and Euler--Heisenberg-type geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27118)
*Bekir Can Lütfüoğlu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带电质量狄拉克场在爱因斯坦-玻恩-因费尔德（BI）精确几何和欧拉-海森堡（EH）近似下的准束缚态，比较了不同约束条件（固定无穷远质量或外视界半径）下非线性效应对频率和衰减行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非线性电动力学（如BI和EH理论）对黑洞背景下量子场准束缚态的影响，特别是与线性麦克斯韦理论的偏离。

Method: 数值求解径向狄拉克方程，在视界处向外积分、在大半径处向内积分，并匹配解以获得复频率；比较BI与EH两种非线性模型在不同参数固定条件下的谱特性。

Result: 当固定质量时，BI与EH对基模实频的修正符号相反；固定视界半径时二者均使实频升高；所有情况下基模衰减均变慢，但最窄能级的衰减行为依赖于固定参数的选择；高阶模响应更复杂；两理论谱随非线性减弱而趋同。

Conclusion: 非线性电动力学对准束缚态的影响具有模型与参数依赖性，不能仅由非线性强度单一刻画；选择不同的物理约束（质量vs.视界半径）会显著改变物理图像。

Abstract: We study quasi-bound states of a charged massive Dirac field in the exact electrically charged Einstein--Born--Infeld (BI) geometry and in a leading-order Euler--Heisenberg-type (EH) approximation. The two descriptions agree in the weak-field limit. We compare families in which either the mass measured at infinity or the outer-horizon radius is held fixed, and examine several field charges and levels. We integrate the radial equations outward from the horizon and inward from large radius, then match the solutions. The resulting frequencies show that the effect of the nonlinear terms depends on both the electrodynamic theory and the chosen comparison. For the fundamental like-charge mode, the small change in its real frequency has opposite signs in the two theories when the mass is fixed, whereas it is upward in both when the horizon radius is fixed. The fundamental mode decays more slowly as the nonlinear term grows along either family. The narrowest level, however, decays more rapidly when the mass is fixed and more slowly when the horizon radius is fixed. The spectra of the two theories approach one another as the nonlinear term is reduced. We also follow several higher levels; their decay widths and their response depend on the state and on the family used for comparison. The nonlinear coefficient is used as an effective parameter and is not matched to the microscopic coupling in quantum electrodynamics (QED).

</details>


### [122] [Regularization effects on the observational appearance of asymmetric thin-shell wormholes from a new string-cloud geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27144)
*Zijun Zhong,Jonathan A. Rebouças,Edson Otoniel,Minyong Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Letelier-Alencar弦云几何的有理正则化如何改变反射不对称薄壳虫洞的光学外观，通过比较不同正则化配置下的光线传播、临界尺度和成像特征，并结合多种发射模型与黑洞对照分析其图像差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解正则化对虫洞时空几何及其可观测光学效应的影响，特别是如何区分传播效应与发射/观测条件对成像对比度的贡献。

Method: 采用临界尺度分析、光线追踪（包括关键射线类、轨道数和传输函数）、三种发射剖面建模，并与匹配的黑洞控制模型对比；考虑面朝向和倾斜视角下，发射体沿类时测地线运动（外区圆轨道+内区能量角动量守恒下落）。

Result: 正则化降低观测者侧临界屏冲击参数（从10.392μ₁降至9.642μ₁）；双正则化进一步压缩反射区间（3.890μ₁→1.248μ₁）；面朝向外边缘发射时正则–正则图像与黑洞控制几乎重合，而内边缘发射可揭示返回结构；大倾角下可解除该近简并。

Conclusion: 正则化显著改变虫洞外部时空与喉部匹配，从而影响光线传播特性；但其成像对比度最终取决于发射源分布、运动状态及观测倾角，需联合建模以分离传播与辐射效应。

Abstract: We investigate how rational regularization of the new Letelier--Alencar string-cloud geometry changes the optical appearance of reflection-asymmetric thin-shell wormholes. We compare junctions with two nonregular exteriors, a regular observer exterior paired with a nonregular one, or two regular exteriors at fixed leading asymptotic masses, throat radius, string strength and regulator fractions. Critical scales, ray classes, orbit numbers and transfer functions are combined with three emission profiles and matched black-hole controls. Face-on and inclined images use the same prescribed emitter motion: circular timelike geodesics outside the innermost stable circular orbit and an inward plunge conserving its specific energy and angular momentum inside. Although the core is excised, regularization changes the retained exterior and throat matching. For the sampled parameters, the observer critical screen impact decreases from $10.392μ_1$ to $9.642μ_1$, where $μ_1$ is the observer-side asymptotic mass scale. Regularizing the second exterior then contracts the reflected interval from $3.890μ_1$ to $1.248μ_1$ without changing ordinary observer-side rays. Returned brightness depends on source support and velocity-dependent frequency shifts: the regular--regular image nearly coincides with its black-hole control for the outer-edge emission model face-on, while inner emission reveals returned structures. At large sampled inclination that near-degeneracy can be lifted. This separates regularization-dependent propagation from the emission and viewing conditions that determine its image contrast.

</details>


### [123] [A Multipole Technique for the Simulation and Analysis of Gravitational Waveforms from Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27153)
*Courtney McIntosh,Jeremy Schnittman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EMRICode，一个用于在Kerr时空下演化极端质量比旋进（EMRI）的Python数值框架，结合了自适应测地线积分与轨道运动常数的长期演化模型，并重点探讨了Carter常数演化的多种方案及其稳定性与偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为准确模拟极端质量比旋进（EMRI）在Kerr时空中的长期轨道演化，特别是处理现有辐射反作用模型中未直接确定的Carter常数演化问题，需构建灵活、模块化的数值框架。

Method: 开发基于Python的EMRICode框架，融合自适应数值积分求解测试粒子测地线与轨道常数（能量、角动量、Carter常数）的世俗演化模型；系统比较多种Carter常数演化方案（尤其径向势能法），并与Peters弱场及文献中Kerr基模型对比验证。

Result: 径向势能法在所考察方案中表现出最优稳定性，但对近共面轨道存在系统性残余偏差；EMRICode成功复现并拓展了经典辐射反作用模型，并支持引入吸积盘相互作用与潮汐撕裂等物理效应。

Conclusion: EMRICode是一个模块化、可扩展的数值工具，不仅适用于理想真空点粒子EMRI建模，也为纳入更复杂天体物理过程（如介质相互作用）提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We introduce EMRICode, a Python-based numerical framework for evolving generic extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) in Kerr spacetime. The code combines adaptive numerical integration of test-particle geodesics with a prescribed model for the secular evolution of the orbital constants of motion, allowing eccentric and inclined trajectories to be followed over long inspiral timescales. We focus in particular on the treatment of the Carter constant, whose evolution is not directly determined by the leading-order fluxes for energy and axial angular momentum used in the present model. We investigate several prescriptions for determining its evolution and find that a radial-potential-based prescription provides the most stable behavior among those considered, while also exhibiting a systematic residual bias for nearly planar orbits. We compare the resulting inspirals with established radiation-reaction approximations, including the weak-field treatment of Peters and Kerr-based prescriptions from the literature, and use these comparisons to assess the qualitative behavior and limitations of the implementation. The modular structure of EMRICode allows the radiation-reaction prescription to be replaced independently of the underlying geodesic evolution, providing a framework for incorporating more accurate flux models and additional physical effects. We present two simple examples of how interactions with accretion disks and partial tidal disruptions can drive the evolution of a stellar EMRI orbit. EMRICode therefore provides a flexible computational framework for studying the orbital dynamics of EMRIs and for exploring extensions beyond the idealized vacuum, point-particle approximation.

</details>


### [124] [The shape of nonlinearity in binary-black-hole dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27164)
*Tousif Islam*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个模块化时域Teukolsky框架，用于研究黑洞微扰理论在有限质量比下的精度限制；发现耗散通量修正可大幅减小非自旋双星的旋进率偏差，但对进动双星和并合阶段效果有限；并指出并合偏移主要源于背景Kerr黑洞与并合残余黑洞的自旋差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞性扰动理论在超出其形式小质量比假设时仍表现出惊人精度，但其精度极限尚不明确。

Method: 构建模块化时域Teukolsky框架，分离有限质量比对轨道动力学的修正，同时保持辐射处于一阶微扰水平，并与数值相对论结果对比分析。

Result: 对于非自旋双星，二阶耗散通量修正消除了87%的旋进率偏差；对进动双星，发现未被标准后牛顿项解释的ν²sin²ι型旋进时间重标度；并合偏移96%由自旋变化引起；发布包含1571个波形的Vishveshwara星表。

Conclusion: 有限质量比效应在不同演化阶段主导来源不同：旋进阶段以耗散修正为主，进动动力学存在新修正项，并合阶段则主要由背景黑洞参数（尤其是自旋）与残余黑洞的失配决定。

Abstract: Black-hole perturbation theory remains remarkably accurate beyond its formal small-mass-ratio regime, but what limits this accuracy remains unclear. We develop a modular time-domain Teukolsky framework that isolates finite-mass-ratio corrections to the orbital dynamics while keeping the radiation at first perturbative order. Comparisons with numerical relativity show that the dominant source of discrepancy changes across the waveform. For nonspinning binaries, the available second-order dissipative flux removes $87\%$ of the inspiral rate discrepancy, bringing it to within $\sim1\%$ of numerical relativity. For precessing binaries, we identify a $ν^2\sin^2ι$ dependence in the inspiral time rescaling that is not explained by the leading orbit-averaged post-Newtonian terms, suggesting finite-mass-ratio corrections to precessional dynamics. Near merger, the same dissipative correction removes only $1$--$2\%$ of the discrepancy. We show instead that the merger offset is quantitatively accounted for by the difference between the initial Kerr background used in the perturbative evolution and the remnant black hole, with $96\%$ of the leading predicted frequency shift arising from the change in spin rather than mass. We release $1571$ time-domain waveforms for $348$ binaries as the Vishveshwara Catalog.

</details>


### [125] [High-cadence GW astrometry in the $μ$Hz gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27221)
*Giorgio Mentasti,Carlo Contaldi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用高速旋转的天体测量卫星探测微赫兹频段随机引力波背景（SGWB）的灵敏度，指出通过提高采样频率可提升在高频段的竞争力，并表明现有类似盖亚（Gaia）的卫星尚不足以探测到脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）已测得的背景，但增大望远镜口径或部署全天覆盖卫星星座可实现该目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）与LISA之间微赫兹频段的随机引力波背景（SGWB）探测空白。

Method: 基于高速自转卫星的天体测量原理，分析其位置测量白噪声与采样频率无关的特性；采用N颗参考恒星的互相关估计器，并在远源极限下计算天体测量重叠函数。

Result: 类似Gaia、扫描黄极、观测10年、含约10^4颗亮星的卫星，其灵敏度比PTA测得的SGWB低约60倍；而8米级大口径望远镜或全天覆盖卫星星座可达到并连续拓展μHz频段探测能力。

Conclusion: 高速自转天体测量巡天是探测微赫兹SGWB的可行途径，需更大口径或星座部署才能达到当前PTA水平，并实现该频段的连续覆盖。

Abstract: We forecast the sensitivity of a dedicated, fast-spinning astrometric survey to a stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) in the microhertz gap between pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) and LISA. Spinning the satellite faster samples each star more often, raising the Nyquist frequency and opening higher frequencies at no cost in noise: the shorter, noisier exposures are exactly compensated by their number, so the white-noise floor of each position stream is independent of cadence. High frequencies are where astrometry is most competitive, because the deflection measures the strain directly: its strain noise is white, whereas the equivalent noise of pulsar timing grows as $f^2$. With a cross-correlation estimator over $N$ reference stars and the astrometric overlap computed in the distant-source limit, we find that a Gaia-like satellite scanning the ecliptic poles, with $N\sim10^4$ bright stars over $10$~yr, falls short of the background measured by PTAs by a factor $\sim\!60$. A larger, $8$-m-class aperture, or a constellation of satellites covering the whole sky, reaches it, while extending the reach continuously across the $μ$Hz band.

</details>


### [126] [Local obstructions to trapped-region escape in modified gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27225)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何优先的方法来分析球对称修正引力中的能量条件，通过精确的面积加权传输定律，推导出捕获持久性定理，并在f(R)引力中构建了基于Wald熵密度的熵分辨层次结构，明确指出物理物质的能量条件而非虚构的有效流体控制熵捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统修正引力中的能量条件通常施加在混合了物质与引力自由度的‘有效’应力张量上，模糊了实际主导零测地线聚焦、视界多重性和捕获区域消失的物理部分。

Method: 在球对称下，利用归一化未来指向径向类光矢量对，推导两个纵向里奇投影和一个独立横向爱因斯坦投影的精确面积加权传输律；在f(R)引力中，基于局部Wald熵密度变量Fr²构建熵分辨层次。

Result: 得出捕获持久性定理；纵向熵聚焦律中纯量场二阶导数精确抵消，仅剩非正梯度平方与物理物质投影；横向熵传输律中所有纯量场导数项全部抵消；熵捕获由物理物质条件T_nn≥0与代数混合阈值决定。

Conclusion: 该方法清晰分离了物质正定性、几何聚焦、边缘面恢复与引力熵演化，精确定义了实现规则内视界、喉部及捕获-反捕获转变所需的局域反转条件。

Abstract: Energy conditions in modified gravity are commonly imposed on an ''effective'' stress tensor that mixes physical matter with additional gravitational degrees of freedom, obscuring which sector actually controls null focusing, horizon multiplicity, and the disappearance of trapped regions. We develop a geometry-first alternative in spherical symmetry. For a normalized pair of future-directed radial null vectors, twolongitudinal Ricci projections and one independent transverse Einstein projection obey exact area-weighted transport laws. These imply a trapping-persistence theorem: after a nondegenerate future-outer marginal sphere, a regular ingoing generator cannot exit the trapped region while the ingoing longitudinal and transverse convergence channels remain nonnegative. In metric $f(R)$ gravity, with $F\equiv df/dR>0$, we then construct an entropy-resolved hierarchy based on the local Wald-density variable $Fr^2$. In the longitudinal entropy-focusing law, the scalaron second derivative cancels exactly, leaving a nonpositive scalar-gradient square together with the physical matter projection; in the independent transverse entropy-transport law, all scalaron derivative terms cancel. Entropy trapping is consequently governed by the physical matter condition $T_{nn}\geq 0$ and an algebraic mixed threshold rather than by a fictitious effective fluid. The resulting hierarchy cleanly separates matter positivity, geometric focusing, marginal-surface recovery, and gravitational-entropy evolution, and identifies the precise local reversals required for regular inner horizons, throats, and trapped-to-antitrapped transitions.

</details>


### [127] [Using Ricci Flow in the Construction of Homogeneous Cosmological Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27340)
*Fan Zhang,Lee Lindblom*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Ricci流与数值求解爱因斯坦方程的方法，用于构建并演化具有非平凡空间拓扑（如Seifert-Weber空间、透镜空间L(5,2)和Sixth-Turn空间）的齐性各向同性宇宙学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究具有非平凡空间拓扑的齐性各向同性宇宙学模型，需构造满足全局齐性的初始数据，而传统方法在此类流形上存在困难。

Method: 引入Ricci流辅助构造全局齐性的初始数据，并通过数值求解爱因斯坦方程对这些初始数据进行演化。

Result: 成功在Seifert-Weber空间、透镜空间L(5,2)和Sixth-Turn空间上构建并演化了齐性各向同性宇宙学模型。

Conclusion: Ricci流是构造非平凡拓扑流形上齐性初始数据的有效工具，该方法可推广至其他紧致三维流形的宇宙学建模。

Abstract: This study develops numerical methods for computing homogeneous and isotropic cosmological models on manifolds with non-trivial spatial topologies. The use of Ricci flow is introduced here to aid in the construction of globally homogeneous initial data on these manifolds. The initial data produced in this way are evolved by solving Einstein's equation numerically to construct homogeneous and isotropic cosmological models on three exemplar manifolds in this study: Seifert-Weber space, the lens space $L(5,2)$, and Sixth-Turn space.

</details>


### [128] [Thermal and optical signatures of a magnetic AdS black hole in dark matter and quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27503)
*Mohsen Fathi,Marco Olivares,Carlos E. Romero-Figueroa,J. R. Villanueva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了被完美流体暗物质和重子物质包围的静态磁反德西特黑洞，分析了其几何结构、热力学性质及观测特征，并通过参数η描述不同配置下的变化趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解暗物质和重子物质对黑洞时空结构及观测信号的影响，特别是对事件视界望远镜（EHT）所观测到的黑洞阴影等现象的修正作用。

Method: 基于广义相对论框架，构建含暗物质与重子物质的静态磁AdS黑洞度规；系统计算霍金温度、光子球、阴影大小、强/弱引力透镜效应、相对论图像等；引入一维参数路径η进行参数扫描，并结合数值测地线验证解析近似。

Result: 随着η从0（Schwarzschild黑洞）增至1（完整模型），外视界、光子球、临界阴影尺度和爱因斯坦环均向外移动；太阳边缘类比给出η≲2.06×10⁻⁵；M87*允许η∈[0,1]，Sgr A*则限制为η≲0.665。

Conclusion: 暗物质与重子物质显著影响黑洞的强场引力特征，尤其在阴影和相对论图像上可被EHT分辨；参数η提供了一种清晰展示多成分耦合效应的方式，但并非物理新耦合；结果支持该模型与当前EHT观测兼容。

Abstract: We study a static magnetic anti-de Sitter black hole surrounded by perfect-fluid dark matter and quintessence. We follow the geometry from the horizon to the observer. We first examine the horizons and curvature. We then calculate the Hawking temperature and sparsity, weak light bending, the photon sphere, the shadow, strong-deflection coefficients, relativistic images, and simple Event Horizon Telescope size intervals. Because the model has several parameters, we use a one-dimensional benchmark path labelled by $η$. The point $η= 0$ is Schwarzschild, while $η= 1$ is the selected full configuration. This path is only a clear way to present the combined changes; it is not a new coupling. We derive the main relations step by step and test the weak- and strong-field approximations with direct numerical geodesics. Along the path, the outer horizon, photon sphere, critical shadow scale, and relativistic Einstein rings move outward. A formal solar-limb comparison gives $η\lesssim 2.06 \times 10^{-5}$ if the same local environment is placed around the Sun, but this is a source-dependent analogue rather than a universal bound. With the central mass and distance values used here, the M87* diameter allows $0 \leq η\leq 1$, while the adopted Sgr A* interval gives $η\lesssim 0.665$. Longer derivations, error propagation, and numerical checks are collected in the appendices.

</details>


### [129] [Untangling Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals and Massive Black Hole Binaries in LISA Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27642)
*Rishav Agrawal,Clémence Allietta,Ollie Burke,Alvin J. K. Chua*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了LISA空间引力波探测器中多个引力波源（如大质量黑洞双星MBHBs和极端质量比旋进EMRIs）联合参数推断问题，发现EMRI与MBHB之间耦合较弱，支持分步推断策略，并验证了不完美MBHB减除对EMRI推断影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA数据流包含多种重叠的引力波信号（如GBs、MBHBs、EMRIs），需发展高效、准确的全局拟合推断策略以应对源间混叠与参数相关性挑战。

Method: 采用贝叶斯推断方法，在模拟LISA数据（含仪器噪声与银河系双星随机背景）中联合分析MBHB与EMRI组合信号；比较完全联合推断与分步（先联合拟合并扣除MBHB，再依次推断EMRI）策略的效果，并评估MBHB减除不完善（如缺失模式或重叠）的影响。

Result: 证实EMRI之间及EMRI与MBHB之间耦合微弱；分步推断策略可行且鲁棒；不完美MBHB减除对EMRI参数恢复影响甚微；联合拟合多个MBHB可有效抑制其推断误差；在含3个MBHB和3个EMRI的模拟数据中，该策略未引入显著参数偏差。

Conclusion: EMRI与MBHB参数推断可解耦，支持层级式、计算高效的分步全局拟合流程，为LISA未来数据分析管道设计提供了关键理论依据与实用策略。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will observe sources in the millihertz regime, such as Galactic Binaries (GBs), Massive Black Hole Binaries (MBHBs), and Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals (EMRIs), so its data stream will be a superposition of an unknown number of gravitational-wave sources. We consider the joint inference of various representative combinations of MBHB and EMRI signals in LISA data (with assumed instrumental noise and stochastic GB foreground). Our Bayesian analyses confirm the expectation of weak inter-source coupling between EMRIs, and between EMRIs and MBHBs, despite strong intra-source parameter correlations. Weak EMRI-MBHB coupling enables a hierarchically sequential approach to EMRI and MBHB inference, rather than fully joint inference. We further show that imperfect MBHB subtraction, arising from missing modes or overlapping MBHBs, has only a weak impact on EMRI inference. For multiple MBHBs, we mitigate inference errors through a joint MBHB fit. We demonstrate this strategy on simulated LISA data containing noise, three MBHBs, and three EMRIs, jointly fitting and subtracting the MBHBs before sequentially inferring the EMRIs - ultimately observing little to no bias on all recovered astrophysical parameters. The notions described throughout our work are highly relevant to informing optimal strategies for the global-fit-based pipelines that are currently being developed for LISA.

</details>


### [130] [Stability Analysis of Axion Dark Energy Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27514)
*Ankan Mukherjee,Nandan Roy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由周期性标量场势驱动的轴子暗能量模型的稳定性，通过动力学系统方法分析了辐射、物质和暗能量主导时期的固定点及其稳定性，发现前两者为鞍点，后者为稳定吸引子，从而保证了宇宙加速膨胀对初值的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子暗能量模型虽能解释晚期宇宙加速膨胀并呈现丰富动力学行为，但其物理可行性高度依赖于是否存在稳定的宇宙学解。

Method: 将背景演化方程重构为自治动力学系统，识别辐射、物质和暗能量主导时期的固定点，并结合解析与数值方法，在观测允许的参数空间内分析其稳定性。

Result: 辐射和物质主导阶段自然表现为鞍点，确保标准宇宙演化；晚期暗能量主导解是稳定吸引子，保障宇宙加速对初值扰动的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 轴子暗能量模型在动力学上自洽且与观测兼容，是解释晚期宇宙加速膨胀的一个可行候选者。

Abstract: We investigate the stability of an axion dark energy model characterized by a periodic scalar-field potential motivated by pseudo Nambu Goldstone bosons. While such models are well suited to drive late-time cosmic acceleration and can exhibit rich dynamical features, including oscillatory behavior of the dark-energy equation-of-state parameter, their viability crucially depends on the existence of stable cosmological solutions. By reformulating the background evolution equations as an autonomous dynamical system, we identify the fixed points corresponding to radiation-dominated, matter-dominated, and dark energy dominated epochs. Using both analytical and numerical techniques, we analyze the stability properties of these fixed points within the observationally allowed region of parameter space. Our results show that the radiation- and matter-dominated phases emerge naturally as saddle points, ensuring a standard cosmological evolution, while the late-time dark energy dominated solution acts as a stable attractor. This guarantees the robustness of cosmic acceleration against variations in initial conditions. Overall, our study establishes the axion dark energy model as a dynamically consistent and observationally viable candidate for explaining the late-time accelerated expansion of the Universe.

</details>


### [131] [Regular Reissner--Nordström--de Sitter Black Holes: Thin-Shell Matching and Modified Density Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27578)
*Reza Saadati,Yu Shi,Vitalii Vertogradov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种正则化Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter黑洞中心奇点的方法：一是通过含时尘埃壳层匹配内外时空，二是修改能量密度分布以构造光滑几何。两种方法均产生de Sitter型正则核心，但机制不同、参数约束不同，且外部几何不可区分奇点与否。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter黑洞中心奇点的物理不合理性，探索可实现的正则化方案，并检验外部观测能否揭示内部奇异性。

Method: 第一种方法采用Israel拼接条件，在de Sitter内区与RN-dS外区之间引入时序尘埃壳层，分析其有效势、静态平衡与径向稳定性；第二种方法直接平滑原始发散的能量密度分布，重构满足渐近RN-dS且具de Sitter核心的光滑时空度规。

Result: 第一种方法导出稳定构型存在的充要条件Λ_->3Λ_+，并给出外质量参数上界；第二种方法中正则化尺度β被唯一确定为Q²/(2m)，无自由调节参数；两种构造不等价，体现不同正则化机制。

Conclusion: 中心奇点可被de Sitter正则核替代而不改变外部可观测几何，因此仅凭外部测量无法判别黑洞内部是否奇异；两类正则化路径本质不同，反映了对奇点问题的不同物理处理思路。

Abstract: We investigate two distinct approaches to regularizing the central singularity of the Reissner--Nordström--de Sitter geometry. In the first approach, a de Sitter interior is matched to an exact Reissner--Nordström--de Sitter exterior across a timelike dust shell. Using the Israel junction conditions, we derive the effective potential governing the shell dynamics and analyze its static equilibrium and radial stability. We show that radial stability requires $Λ_->Λ_+$, while the additional requirement of three distinct exterior horizons, with the shell located inside the inner horizon, leads to the stronger analytical result that such stable black-hole configurations exist if and only if $Λ_->3Λ_+$. We also derive an upper bound on the exterior mass parameter and recover the Reissner--Nordström limit as $Λ_+\to0$. Although the singular center is replaced by a regular core, the stationary exterior geometry remains unchanged, demonstrating that exterior geometric observations alone cannot determine whether the black-hole interior is singular or regular. In the second approach, we modify the divergent energy-density profile and reconstruct a smooth geometry with a de Sitter core while preserving the Reissner--Nordström--de Sitter asymptotics. Regularity fixes the smoothing scale to $β=Q^2/(2m)$, leaving no independent regularization parameter. The two constructions thus provide distinct regularization mechanisms, rather than equivalent descriptions of the same spacetime.

</details>


### [132] [Geometric Quantum Discord in Schwarzschild de Sitter Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27887)
*Xukun Wang,Tinggui Zhang,Xiaofen Huang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwarzschild-de Sitter时空下无质量Dirac场模的几何量子失谐，重点分析黑洞与宇宙学视界热效应的差异影响，发现黑洞质量、频率及初态结构共同调控可访问与不可访问失谐的行为特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究弯曲时空中不同视界（黑洞与宇宙学视界）热效应对量子关联（几何量子失谐）的差异化影响，理解引力与量子效应在量子信息中的耦合作用。

Method: 基于Schwarzschild-de Sitter时空中的无质量Dirac场模，计算纯Bell型和各向同性初态下的几何量子失谐，区分可访问与不可访问模式，系统分析黑洞质量、宇宙学常数和无量纲频率等参数的影响。

Result: 1) 增加黑洞质量可恢复可访问失谐；2) 高频更利于失谐保持；3) 不可访问失谐对模式选择敏感，且频率依赖关系随黑洞质量变化而反转；4) 在Nariai极限下，可访问失谐恢复初值，所有不可访问失谐消失。

Conclusion: 量子失谐的动力学由视界热效应、场模选取和初态结构三者协同决定，揭示了强引力场中量子关联的复杂调控机制。

Abstract: We investigate geometric quantum discord for massless Dirac field modes in Schwarzschild-de Sitter spacetime, focusing on the distinct thermal effects of the black hole and cosmological horizons. For pure Bell-type and isotropic initial states, increasing the black hole mass at fixed cosmological constant restores accessible discord, while higher dimensionless frequencies favor its preservation. Inaccessible discord depends on the retained modes: higher frequencies suppress discord involving the inaccessible cosmological mode, whereas the frequency ordering reverses with increasing black hole mass for the inaccessible black hole mode paired with the accessible cosmological mode. For the selected parameters yielding equal initial discord, the two state families exhibit identical discord in sectors containing the inaccessible black hole mode but differ over part of the parameter range in the remaining inaccessible sector. At fixed positive dimensionless frequency, accessible discord returns to its initial value in the Nariai limit, while all three inaccessible discords vanish. These results reveal the interplay between horizon thermal effects, mode selection, and initial-state structure in shaping quantum correlations.

</details>


### [133] [Thin-Shell Wormholes in Born--Infeld-Type Magnetic Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28112)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,Edson Otoniel,Muhammad Israr Aslam,Rabia Saleem*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Born-Infeld型磁外区构建的对称薄壳虫洞的径向稳定性、热力学平衡与双侧盘面成像特性，发现其稳定性依赖于状态方程（如修正Chaplygin气体律），并揭示了磁自能、电荷尺度、光线传播及吸积盘运动对虫洞结构与观测特征的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性电磁理论（Born-Infeld型）下薄壳虫洞的物理可行性，尤其关注其稳定性机制、能量条件满足情况及可观测的吸积盘图像特征，以区分几何、物质与运动学效应对虫洞支撑和辐射观测的贡献。

Method: 采用广义相对论中薄壳形式主义，结合精确的lapse函数与拼接条件构建固定质量虫洞族；分析线性径向扰动下的稳定性；推导光学极限下麦克斯韦光线在透明拼接界面的传播；模拟静态、圆周及 plunging 轨道下吸积盘的强度图像；并基于可逆第一定律讨论热力学行为。

Result: 发现壳层虽具负能量密度，但内采样区可满足部分能量条件；特定Chaplygin类状态方程下存在稳定区域；修正线性压强项可使稳定性局限于有限径向区间；亚临界电荷减小临界圆半径，增强盘面光线穿越次数；静止观测者看到的盘面强度受喉部切除影响显著；运动学（尤其是倾角）引入强方位对比；通量抵消导致正温下等熵运动。

Conclusion: Born-Infeld磁虫洞在特定物态与参数范围内具备动力学稳定性与可观测性，其热力学与成像特征由磁自能标度、壳层本构关系及吸积流运动状态共同决定，为检验高能非线性电磁理论提供了潜在引力探针。

Abstract: We investigate the radial stability, thermodynamic balance, and bilateral disk images of symmetric thin-shell wormholes built from Born--Infeld-type magnetic exteriors. The exact lapse and junction conditions define a fixed-mass family in which magnetic self-energy ties the nonlinear scale to the charge. The shell has negative energy density, although an inner sampled region satisfies the tangential null and intrinsic strong energy conditions. Linear radial perturbations yield unstable calibrated barotropic samples and stable sectors for the generalized and modified cosmic Chaplygin laws on selected real branches. Analytic horizon and large-radius limits explain the stability transitions and show how the modified linear pressure term can replace outer stability with finite radial intervals. Minimally coupled Maxwell rays traverse transparent junctions between exteriors with a common arctangent emission profile, viewed by a static observer. The wormhole-black-hole intensity difference separates transmitted disk light from emission removed by throat excision. Compared with static emitters, circular and plunging motion reduce the face-on intensities in the studied cases; inclined motion generates strong azimuthal contrast. Increasing the subcritical charge contracts the critical circle and permits more disk crossings near it in the optical samples. Under a reversible first law, flux cancellation gives isentropic motion at positive temperature, with entropy dependence left to a constitutive completion. These results distinguish geometric, material, and kinematic effects on shell support and disk illumination.

</details>


### [134] [On the Stability of Tolman Oppenheimer Volkoff Fireholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28140)
*Pablo Laguna,Octavio Guerrero,Kostas Kokkotas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有辐射状态方程的奇异Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff（TOV）火洞的径向稳定性，发现其在未受限时是不稳定的，只有当被限制在其火洞半径内时才可能稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有裸奇点和特殊质量分布的奇异TOV火洞的径向稳定性，特别是其在不同边界条件下的稳定性阈值行为。

Method: 采用Prüfer相位法识别基本径向模式，并考虑两种外边界条件（零拉格朗日压强扰动和零径向位移），结合变分界限和TOV方程的缩放对称性进行分析。

Result: 发现开放火洞的临界半径R_c^O小于平衡火洞半径R，而封闭火洞的临界半径R_c^B近似等于R；且在大中心质量极限下R_c^B趋近于R；刚性壁是最具稳定性的分离自伴外边界条件，无界构型则不稳定。

Conclusion: TOV火洞在径向上是不稳定的，除非被限制在约其火洞半径之内。

Abstract: We investigate the radial stability of singular Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff fireholes with a radiation equation of state. These configurations contain a naked, negative point-mass singularity surrounded by a positive-density fluid, whose mass profile develops a Schwarzschild-like plateau before approaching an extended, self-similar atmosphere. Using the Prüfer phase method to identify the fundamental radial mode, we consider two outer boundary conditions: vanishing Lagrangian pressure perturbation (open firehole) and vanishing radial displacement (boxed firehole). For both, the fundamental squared frequency changes from positive to negative as the outer boundary exceeds a critical radius $R_c$. We find that $R_c$ follows approximately the same scaling with the central mass parameter as the equilibrium firehole radius $R$. However, the stability thresholds differ significantly: $R_c^{\rm O}<R$ for the open firehole, whereas $R_c^{\rm B}\simeq R$ for the boxed one. The regular zero mode is the derivative of the equilibrium family with respect to the inner lapse normalization at fixed central mass. This gives the boxed threshold a turning-point interpretation and, together with a scaling symmetry of the TOV equations, implies $R_c^{\rm B}\to R$ in the limit of large central mass. Variational bounds show that the rigid wall is the most stabilizing separated, self-adjoint outer boundary condition and that the unbounded configuration is unstable. Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff fireholes are therefore radially unstable unless confined within approximately their firehole radius.

</details>


### [135] [Topological ModMax--AdS Black Holes: Extremality, Charge Normalization, and Limits of WGC Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28359)
*Saeed Noori Gashti,Behnam Pourhassan,Saheb Soroushfar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由ModMax电动力学及其符号反转版本所激发的四维拓扑反德西特黑洞，明确了电荷归一化、势和热力学第一定律，并分类了球形、平面和双曲视界下的精确极值根；特别指出双曲ModMax分支无上限电荷判别界限，并存在有限电荷零质量点；还推导了不稳定的球形类光圆轨道，并计算了特定带电标量探针在近视界AdS₂中的Breitenlohner-Freedman指数；但这些仅为经典诊断，不能作为弱引力猜想（WGC）的判决依据，且无法用当前固定耦合的AdS数据定量检验距离与德西特猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究ModMax电动力学及其符号反转版本在四维拓扑AdS黑洞背景下的物理行为，特别是电荷归一化、极值结构、稳定性及与量子引力猜想（如WGC、距离/德西特猜想）的潜在联系。

Method: 基于广义电动力学模型（ModMax及其符号反转），求解Einstein-Maxwell型场方程，得到精确黑洞解；通过分析度规函数根确定极值条件；计算类光圆轨道半径与近视界AdS₂中带电标量场的BF界；强调经典诊断与量子引力判据之间的区别。

Result: 1) 给出了球、平、双曲拓扑下AdS黑洞的精确极值电荷条件；2) 发现双曲ModMax黑洞无电荷上限，且存在有限电荷零质量点；3) 得到不稳定球形类光圆轨道；4) 计算了指定带电标量探针的近视界BF指数；5) 指出当前模型无法支持WGC或距离/德西特猜想的定量检验。

Conclusion: ModMax类电动力学可产生丰富而特殊的AdS黑洞结构，但其经典解本身不足以判定量子引力猜想的成立与否；需引入粒子谱、紫外完备性或全局扰动分析才能进一步检验相关猜想。

Abstract: We study four-dimensional topological anti-de Sitter black holes sourced by ModMax electrodynamics and its sign-reversed counterpart. Distinguishing the field-strength amplitude from the conserved displacement flux fixes the charge normalization, potential, and first law. We classify the exact extremal roots for spherical, planar, and hyperbolic horizons; in particular, the hyperbolic ModMax branch has no upper-charge discriminant bound and contains a finite-charge zero-mass point. We also derive the unstable spherical null circular orbit and evaluate the local near-horizon AdS$_2$ Breitenlohner--Freedman index for an explicitly specified charged-scalar probe. These are classical diagnostics, not a WGC verdict: the model does not specify a charged-particle spectrum, an ultraviolet completion, or the global perturbation problem. The distance and de Sitter conjectures cannot be quantitatively tested with the fixed-coupling AdS data considered here.

</details>


### [136] [Geometrically Induced Semiclassical Limit for Quasinormal Modes and Greybody Factors in Higher-Dimensional Near-Ultracold spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28367)
*Bogeun Gwak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter黑洞中拟正则模（QNMs）与灰体因子（GBFs）之间的对应关系，重点关注近超冷态下三 horizon 接近时的情形；在几何极限下，主导阶WKB近似给出QNMs，并保持GBFs对两个参数的显式依赖；进一步揭示了GBF半透射频率及其导数与测地线（如角频率、Lyapunov指数）之间的普适关系，并在任意维数下给出表达式；还探讨了Nariai极限及近超冷但horizon分离情形下的高阶修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维带电宇宙学黑洞中拟正则模与灰体因子的深层物理联系，特别是在近超冷极限下多horizon结构对辐射特性和时空几何的影响。

Method: 采用WKB近似方法，在几何极限而非eikonal极限下计算拟正则模；结合灰体因子解析表达式，分析其半透射频率及导数，并关联至null geodesics相关量（如角频率和Lyapunov指数）；在Nariai极限和近超冷但horizon分离两种几何下研究高阶修正。

Result: 1) 在几何极限下，QNMs由主导阶WKB项主导，且GBFs保持对两个关键参数的显式依赖；2) 半透射频率及其导数被证明与null geodesics的角频率和Lyapunov指数直接相关，并给出了任意维数下的普适关系；3) 给出了Nariai极限和近超冷分离horizon情形下的高阶QNM修正结果。

Conclusion: QNMs与GBFs在近超冷高维RN-dS黑洞中存在深刻而普适的几何联系，该联系通过null geodesics得以体现，且不依赖于eikonal近似，拓展了黑洞微扰理论与散射性质之间的桥梁。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the correspondence between quasinormal modes (QNMs) and greybody factors (GBFs) in higher-dimensional Reissner--Nordstöm--de Sitter black holes based on the massless scalar field. Our analysis focuses on the QNMs in the near-ultracold family where the inner, outer, and cosmological horizons are close together. Under the geometric limit condition, the leading-order Wentzel--Kramers--Brillouin term of the QNM is dominant for fixed angular quantum numbers and dimensionality without imposing the eikonal limit. Particularly, the corresponding GBFs also retain the explicit form of both parameters. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the half-transmission frequency of the GBF and its derivative are related to null geodesics, in terms of aspects such as the angular frequency and Lyapunov exponent, with the use of both parameters. We provide their relationships in arbitrary dimensionality. Furthermore, the higher-order corrections to the QNMs are investigated at the Nariai limit and in the near-ultracold geometry with separated horizons.

</details>


### [137] [Quantum Bianchi IX Anisotropies as Inflationary Relics in the Primordial Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28436)
*Vittorio D'Alelio,Giovanni Montani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从Wheeler-DeWitt方程出发，利用Born-Oppenheimer分离和WKB半经典展开，研究量子各向异性能否作为暴胀前几何的遗迹存留并影响原初曲率谱；发现原初功率谱中存在一个依赖于Bianchi IX量子态的各向异性项，其源于各向异性的量子涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子各向异性能否作为暴胀前量子几何的遗迹，在暴胀后仍留下可观测印记（尤其在原初曲率谱中）

Method: 基于Wheeler-DeWitt方程，采用Born-Oppenheimer波泛函分离（慢引力+快物质），并在大Planck质量参数M下做WKB半经典展开（至M^{-1}阶）；引力部分取de Sitter背景加小量Bianchi IX各向异性，物质为单标量暴胀子场。

Result: 在M^{-1}阶得到含各向异性波函数的量子引力修正项；原初曲率功率谱包含两个可区分成分：一个与各向异性无关，另一个显式依赖Bianchi IX量子态，并由各向异性量子涨落维持。

Conclusion: 预暴胀时期的量子各向异性可通过其量子涨落机制，在原初曲率谱中留下可观测的各向异性印记，从而提供探测早期量子引力效应的新途径。

Abstract: Starting from the WheelerDeWitt equation, we investigate whether quantum anisotropies can survive inflation as relics of the preinflationary geometry and leave an imprint on the primordial curvature spectrum. We perform a BornOppenheimer separation of the wave functional into slow gravitational and fast matter components, justified by the hierarchy between the Planck scale and the lower matter scale, associated here with the inflaton. The full wave functional is then treated through a semiclassical WKB expansion in M to the minus 1, where M is quadratic in the Planck mass. The gravitational sector consists of a de Sitter background supplemented by homogeneous quantum anisotropies in the smallanisotropy regime of the Bianchi IX potential, while matter is represented by a single scalar inflaton field. At order M in the expansion parameter, the WheelerDeWitt equation reproduces the vacuum HamiltonJacobi equation with a cosmological constant. At order M to 0, the internal freedom of the BornOppenheimer factorization separates quantum field theory on curved spacetime from the anisotropy dynamics, described by timedependent harmonic oscillators. At order M to the minus 1, we obtain the quantumgravitational correction to matter. The anisotropy wave function enters explicitly the corresponding operator, so the corrected dynamics retains information about the Bianchi IX quantum state. The curvature power spectrum consequently contains two distinguishable contributions: one independent of the anisotropy state and a genuinely anisotropic term. The latter survives through the quantum variance of the anisotropies, thereby identifying the mechanism through which the preinflationary quantum geometry can affect the primordial spectrum.

</details>


### [138] [Finite-range hair from a nonlocal matter sector: charged Yukawa black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28451)
*Francisco Tello-Ortiz,Hishan Farfán-Bachiloglu,Alejandro Rueda,M. Zubair*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个新的精确带电、静态、球对称黑洞解，其度规为带有Yukawa型修正的Reissner-Nordström几何，在强场区可见但无独立渐近荷；系统分析了其极端曲线、高阶退化视界条件、奇点性质、能量条件、热力学、光子球、阴影、成像及拟正规模，并利用EHT对M87*和Sgr A*的观测约束了该有限程荷的参数空间，支持其强屏蔽、近RN极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义相对论中带电黑洞除质量、电荷外是否可携带有限程‘毛发’，并建立其几何与可观测现象的统一刻画。

Method: 解析求解Einstein-Maxwell方程耦合各向异性物质（Yukawa型）；分析极端曲线、能量条件、热力学变量与广义Smarr关系；计算光子球、阴影、射线追踪图像及六阶WKB+谱方法验证的拟正规模。

Result: 获得含指数屏蔽Yukawa项的精确黑洞解；给出极端曲线解析表达式；确认满足弱、零、主、强能量条件；导出含Yukawa幅值与力程共轭变量的广义热力学；EHT阴影观测直接限制该有限程荷，倾向强屏蔽近RN极限。

Conclusion: 有限程带电‘毛发’可在纯爱因斯坦引力中自洽存在，其效应局域于强场区，不破坏渐近结构；EHT观测已具备区分此类新物理的能力，为检验超越RN的黑洞结构提供了新途径。

Abstract: { We construct a new exact charged, static and spherically symmetric black-hole solution of the Einstein--Maxwell equations with an additional anisotropic matter sector, whose metric is the Reissner--Nordström geometry endowed with a Yukawa-type contribution. The Yukawa term is exponentially screened: it is visible in the strong-field region but carries no independent asymptotic charge. We obtain an analytic parametrization of the extremal curve, identify the condition for higher-order degenerate horizons, characterize the curvature singularity and establish the weak, null, dominant and strong energy conditions. We further study the horizon thermodynamics, including the conjugate variables associated with the amplitude and range of the Yukawa sector and the corresponding generalized Smarr relation. The photon sphere, shadow, ray-traced appearance and quasinormal ringdown are then analyzed, with quasinormal frequencies computed through the sixth-order WKB expansion and confirmed, overtones included, by an independent spectral method. {Confronting the model shadow with Event Horizon Telescope measurements of M87* and Sgr A* constrains the finite-range sector directly, favoring its strongly screened, near-Reissner--Nordström regime.} This provides a unified geometric and phenomenological characterization of finite-range charged black-hole hair in ordinary Einstein gravity. }

</details>


### [139] [Monopole stability of time-dependent, spherically symmetric backgrounds in Horndeski theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28592)
*K. Kiselev,V. Volkova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了最一般的Horndeski理论中关于任意时变球对称背景的线性扰动，重点分析偶极（ℓ=0）扰动以约束标量场自由度，并推导其二次主作用量及稳定性判据，补充了奇宇称扰动分析的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇宇称扰动分析无法约束标量场自由度，需通过偶宇称单极（ℓ=0）扰动来完整刻画该自由度并建立稳定性条件。

Method: 推导偶宇称单极扰动下的标量场传播模式的二次主作用量，并建立消除幽灵不稳定性与径向梯度不稳定的高能模稳定性判据。

Result: 得到了适用于一般时变球对称背景的单极扰动稳定性条件，复现了静态与宇宙学极限下的已知结果；在Horndeski三次子类中，单极判据未带来额外限制；应用于非最小耦合标量场与含线性时变标量毛的黑洞模型时，单极条件进一步缩小了奇宇称稳定性所允许的参数空间。

Conclusion: 偶宇称单极扰动分析为Horndeski理论中时变球对称背景的稳定性提供了必要且互补的判据，完善了此前仅依赖奇宇称分析的局限性。

Abstract: We study linear perturbations about an arbitrary time-dependent, spherically symmetric background in the most general Horndeski theory. The odd-parity sector of such perturbations, analyzed previously, contains no scalar field perturbation and, hence, leaves the related degree of freedom unconstrained. In this paper, we address this issue by analyzing even-parity monopole ($\ell = 0$) perturbations, which isolate this degree of freedom. We derive the quadratic master action for the only propagating physical mode associated with the scalar field and formulate stability criteria which ensure the absence of ghost and radial gradient instabilities for the high-energy modes. These conditions are necessary for the stability of the background and complement the odd-parity ones. We show that they reproduce the known results in the static and cosmological limits. We also demonstrate that, in the cubic subclass of Horndeski theory on a dynamical background, the monopole criteria impose no constraints beyond those previously obtained for modes with large multipoles ($\ell \gg 1$). As an illustration, we apply the derived stability conditions for the monopole mode to a non-minimally coupled scalar field theory and to a black hole model with linearly time-dependent scalar hair; in the latter case, the monopole conditions further restrict the parameter space allowed by odd-parity stability.

</details>


### [140] [Universal Structure of Horizon Formation in Generic Binary Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11819)
*Yu-Cun Xie,Vaishak Prasad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了双黑洞并合中首个共同表观视界的局部结构，发现其形成过程具有普适性，并通过Lyapunov-Schmidt约化得到平方根分支分离与线性漂移叠加的‘倾斜抛物线’行为；数值模拟验证了该理论预测的标度律（误差<0.5%）及形成时刻精度（~5×10⁻⁵M），并提示该事件可能在引力波波形中留下可探测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解双黑洞并合中首个共同表观视界（即共同视界）的形成机制，该事件处于完全非线性区域，超出后牛顿理论和黑洞微扰理论的标准适用范围，但可能存在普适描述。

Method: 基于无对称性假设，利用外陷面稳定性算子失逆性、主本征值正则性及Lyapunov-Schmidt约化方法，推导视界形成附近的几何与物理量演化；并通过三组不同参数（偏心、进动、质量不等）的数值相对论模拟进行验证。

Result: 理论预测共同视界形成附近其横截面分离满足(t−t_*)^{1/2}标度律，且存在共享线性漂移项；数值结果证实世界管几何与类局域标量均符合该标度，自由指数拟合误差小于0.5%，形成时刻诊断一致性达5×10⁻⁵M；还发现视界剪切与引力波‘news’之间存在强相关性。

Conclusion: 首个共同表观视界的形成具有普适的局部结构，其动力学由平方根奇异性主导，该特性不仅被高精度数值模拟严格验证，还可能为引力波观测提供新的并合阶段识别依据。

Abstract: We derive the local structure of the first common apparent horizon in a generic binary-black-hole merger. This event occurs in the fully nonlinear regime, outside the standard regimes of post-Newtonian inspiral theory and perturbations of a stationary black hole, yet it admits a universal description. Without assuming symmetry, we show that the stability operator of the marginally outer trapped surface must lose invertibility at formation. Outermost stability then implies that the vanishing eigenvalue is the principal one, with a strictly positive eigenfunction. Lyapunov-Schmidt reduction yields square-root branch separation with a shared linear drift. Together these terms give a tilted parabola through linear order in time. The common horizon lies on a smooth marginally outer trapped tube tangent to the formation slice, and nearby later slices intersect it in outer and inner branches whose separation scales as $(t-t_*)^{1/2}$. Horizon quantities with a nonzero first response along the zero mode inherit the square-root separation and a shared linear term. We test these predictions in three binary black hole simulations, including an eccentric, precessing, unequal-mass system. In all three, the worldtube geometry and quasilocal scalars follow the predicted scaling. With the next-order term included, free-exponent fits to the surface geometry and quasilocal functionals recover $1/2$ to within half a percent, and diagnostics agree on the formation time within $5\times10^{-5}M$. The correlation between horizon shear and gravitational-wave news suggests that common horizon formation could have a signature in a short segment of the merger waveform.

</details>
