Tarsitano,G. Testera,R. Teyssier,S. Tosi,A. Troja,C. Uhlemann,A. Venhola,D. Vergani,S. Vinciguerra,M. von Wietersheim-Kramsta,L. Wang,A. H. Wright,H. W. Yeung,D. Scott*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于欧几里得（Euclid）星系团聚测量的模拟框架，通过简化仪器和噪声模型，高效模拟一维无狭缝光谱（FastSpec）和检测概率（Pypelid），并校准了选择函数、红移误差及干扰源，验证其在星系二维相关函数恢复中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为避免全像素级模拟的巨大计算开销，同时高精度刻画Euclid光谱巡天的选择效应与系统误差，需构建高效且物理合理的模拟框架以支撑星系团聚科学分析。

Method: 构建双工具框架：FastSpec模拟1维 slitless 光谱（含简化仪器与噪声模型）；Pypelid基于输入星表计算检测概率，纳入巡天dithering序列与空间变化噪声；并开发校准的选择函数、红移误差分布与互扰源（如[OIII]、[SIII]误识为Hα）模型。

Result: 对Euclid宽场巡天基准选择，红移纯度74%、完备率45%，引入测光颜色后提升；红移误差主成分为σ_z = 9.4×10⁻⁴的高斯核，0.9<z<1.8内无显著演化；[OIII]与[SIII]误识贡献数至十余个百分点；低信噪比下灾难性红移失败主导；DR1区域模拟表明前向建模随机样本可有效校正选择效应，恢复相关函数单极与四极。

Conclusion: 该框架为Euclid光谱选择函数提供了高效、达科学精度的表征手段，是其星系团聚系统误差评估的基础，后续将结合DR1实测数据改进噪声模型（如重叠光谱残余污染等）。

Abstract: We present a simulation framework for Euclid galaxy clustering measurements that reproduces the essential behaviour of the spectroscopic reduction chain, bypassing the computation required by full pixel-level simulations. The framework comprises two complementary tools, FastSpec, which simulates 1-dimensional slitless spectra with a simplified instrument and noise model, and Pypelid, which computes detection probabilities on input catalogues, accounting for the survey dithering sequence and spatially varying noise. We develop calibrated prescriptions for the survey selection function, redshift errors, and redshift interloper populations. Applied to the Euclid Wide Survey, the baseline selection yields a redshift purity of 74% and a sample completeness of 45%, which improves with the use of Euclid photometric colours. The redshift error distribution is found to have a Gaussian core of width $σ_z = 9.4 \times 10^{-4}$, with no significant trend over $0.9 < z < 1.8$. Emission lines [OIII]5008 and [SIII]9530 misidentified as H$α$ contribute a few to ten per cent depending on redshift, while catastrophic redshift measurement failures dominate at low signal-to-noise ratio. Simulating the Data Release 1 footprint, we find that complex survey selection effects can be corrected with a forward-modelled random catalogue, recovering the monopole and quadrupole of the galaxy 2-point correlation function. The simplified noise model does not include residual contamination from overlapping spectra, or other artefacts seen in early Euclid data; this will be revisited with DR1 data. The framework provides an efficient route for characterising the Euclid spectroscopic selection function at the precision required for galaxy clustering science, and is the basis for systematic error assessments in Euclid galaxy clustering analyses.

</details>


### [142] [Gamma-ray Bursts as Cosmological Probes Calibrated with Strongly Lensed Quasars and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27009)
*Wei Hong,Luca Izzo,Massimo Della Valle,Orlando Luongo,Marco Muccino,Tong-Jie Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用强引力透镜类星体作为外部校准器，校准伽马射线暴（GRB）的Amati和Combo关系，通过人工神经网络重建光度距离关系，并在平直ΛCDM模型下进行分层贝叶斯分析，得到哈勃常数H₀和物质密度参数Ωₘ的约束，但结果仍依赖TDCOSMO的距离标度，精度受限于GRB弥散、红移相关系统误差及透镜建模不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伽马射线暴（GRBs）可延伸至比Ia型超新星更高的红移，但其宇宙学应用受限于校准循环性和可能的红移依赖效应。

Method: 使用TDCOSMO强引力透镜距离训练人工神经网络以重建光度距离关系，仅采用透镜支持红移区间内的GRBs避免外推；对平直ΛCDM及w₀wₐCDM模型开展分层贝叶斯分析；辅以注入-回收检验评估系统偏差。

Result: 平直ΛCDM下，Amati关系给出H₀=78.26⁺¹⁷.⁸⁷₋₁₆.₃₀ km·s⁻¹·Mpc⁻¹、Ωₘ=0.670⁺⁰.²²³₋₀.₂₇₆；Combo关系给出H₀=78.71⁺¹³.⁸³₋₁₁.₂₉ km·s⁻¹·Mpc⁻¹、Ωₘ=0.635⁺⁰.²³⁵₋₀.₂₄₂；Combo对红移项更敏感，未建模的正向趋势会导致H₀与Ωₘ偏高。

Conclusion: 强透镜校准为拓展GRB哈勃图提供了可行路径，但所得H₀并非独立的GRB测量，仍继承TDCOSMO距离标度；精度主要受GRB本征弥散、红移相关系统误差及透镜建模不确定性限制。

Abstract: Gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) extend distance measurements to redshifts beyond those routinely probed by Type Ia supernovae, but their use in cosmology is limited by calibration circularity and possible redshift-dependent effects. We investigate strongly lensed quasars as external calibrators of the Amati and Combo GRB relations. We reconstruct the luminosity-distance relation using an artificial neural network trained on TDCOSMO strong-lensing distances, with the absolute scale inherited from the lensing-only calibration. To avoid extrapolation, only GRBs within the lens-supported redshift interval are used for calibration. We then infer cosmological parameters through a hierarchical Bayesian analysis of flat $Λ$CDM, with $w_0w_a$CDM examined as a consistency test. For flat $Λ$CDM, Amati yields $H_0=78.26^{+17.87}_{-16.30}\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ and $Ω_m=0.670^{+0.223}_{-0.276}$, while Combo yields $H_0=78.71^{+13.83}_{-11.29}\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ and $Ω_m=0.635^{+0.235}_{-0.242}$. Amati is relatively insensitive to the phenomenological redshift term, whereas Combo shows a stronger dependence. Injection--recovery tests indicate that an unmodelled positive Combo trend can bias $H_0$ and $Ω_m$ upward. Strong-lens calibration therefore offers a route to extending the GRB Hubble diagram, but the inferred $H_0$ is not an independent GRB measurement: it retains the TDCOSMO distance scale. The precision is limited by GRB scatter, redshift-dependent systematics, and lens-modelling uncertainties.

</details>


### [143] [ASTROLABE: A 3D Particle-Level Surrogate for Cosmology Simulation with Physical Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27068)
*Ibrahim Elsharkawy,David Curtin,Yonatan Kahn,Neal Weiner*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出Astrolabe，一种基于流匹配的生成式模拟器，用于高效、高分辨率地模拟暗物质宇宙学结构，克服了传统N体模拟计算昂贵和现有机器学习模拟器分辨率低或适用范围窄的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统N体模拟在暗物质理论空间进行系统性扫描计算成本过高；现有机器学习模拟器受限于低分辨率或仅能模拟特定可观测量。

Method: Astrolabe采用基于流匹配的生成建模方法，直接作用于N个粒子的6N维相空间坐标；引入场-粒子混合Transformer架构实现线性计算复杂度；以近似COLA坐标为物理先验，并全程约束动量与质量守恒。

Result: 在ΛCDM模型测试中，Astrolabe成功复现了准确的功率谱、晕质量函数、速度轮廓和密度轮廓；展现出对暗声学振荡初条件的分布外泛化能力；并可通过微调高效模拟大规模（如TNG50-3-dark）系统。

Conclusion: Astrolabe是迈向通用、强大宇宙学模拟代理的重要第一步，未来有望纳入重子物理并系统探索更丰富的暗区相互作用。

Abstract: The microphysics of dark matter (DM) is imprinted on cosmological structure across an enormous dynamic range. Nonlinear cosmological observables are most reliably modeled with N-body and hydrodynamic simulations. However, systematic N-body scans over DM theory space are prohibitively expensive. Machine learning emulators provide a possible solution, but are currently limited by either low resolution or only the ability to emulate specific observables. We introduce Astrolabe, a flow-matching-based generative emulator designed to be free of both constraints. Astrolabe acts directly on the 6N phase-space coordinates of N individual particles. With no voxel grid in the output, there is no voxel-resolution ceiling, and in principle any observable can be measured from inference. Astrolabe is computationally tractable via a field-particle hybrid transformer in which every particle cross-attends to multiscale field tokens, so compute and memory are linear in N. Astrolabe incorporates physical flow matching by taking the prior distribution for the flow to be approximate COmoving Lagrangian Acceleration (COLA)-based coordinates rather than unphysical noise, with momentum and mass conservation enforced at all points along flow time. In the Lambda-CDM cases we test, Astrolabe produces realistic fields with accurate power spectra, halo mass functions, velocity profiles, and halo density profiles. We also observe out-of-distribution generalization, with accurate emulation of dark-acoustic oscillation initial conditions. Astrolabe is also capable of being fine-tuned to simulate very large N and we demonstrate this by emulating a TNG50-3-dark simulation at a fraction of the cost.This method represents a first step toward a general and powerful cosmology simulation surrogate, which may eventually permit the inclusion of baryonic physics and a systematic exploration of richer dark sector interactions.

</details>


### [144] [DDELenS: Tomographic Weak Lensing Simulations with Dynamical Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27088)
*Shu-Fan Chen,J. Colin Hill,Zoltán Haiman*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一套包含128个在w0waCDM模型下、含大质量中微子的宇宙学模拟，旨在支持基于模拟的弱引力透镜等观测对动力学暗能量参数的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持下一代弱引力透镜巡天（如LSST、Euclid、Roman）和CMB透镜实验（如Simons Observatory）对动力学暗能量参数（w0, wa）的高精度场级推断与非高斯统计分析。

Method: 采用Sobol序列在Ωm、σ8、w0、wa参数空间内采样，运行128个N体模拟（1024³粒子，512 h⁻¹Mpc盒子），使用PKDGRAV3（N体规范）处理含中微子、光子及暗能量的相对论效应；用LensTools生成每组10000张6个源红移处的光线追踪汇合度图，并提供晕表与投影晕过密度场；在标称宇宙学下生成100000次实现以构建协方差矩阵。

Result: 产出128组高分辨率、含相对论修正的N体模拟数据集，每组含大量弱透镜收敛图、晕目录及投影场，支持弱透镜与星系交叉相关分析，并提供高精度协方差矩阵。

Conclusion: 该模拟套件为利用下一代宇宙学巡天数据开展基于模拟的场级贝叶斯推断、非高斯统计建模及动力学暗能量参数约束提供了关键数值基础设施。

Abstract: We present a suite of 128 cosmological simulations in $w_0w_a$CDM cosmologies with massive neutrinos, designed to enable simulation-based inference of dynamical dark energy parameters from weak gravitational lensing and other cosmological observables. Each simulation follows $1024^3$ dark matter particles in a $512\,h^{-1}$Mpc periodic box, with cosmological parameters sampled using a Sobol sequence over the ranges $Ω_m \in [0.28, 0.35]$, $σ_8 \in [0.74, 0.90]$, $w_0 \in [-1.0, -0.5]$, and $w_a \in [-1.5, 0.0]$, motivated by recent results from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument survey. The nonlinear evolution is computed with $\texttt{PKDGRAV3}$ using the N-body gauge to consistently incorporate relativistic corrections from massive neutrinos, photons, and dark energy. For each cosmology, we generate 10,000 ray-traced convergence maps at six source redshifts $z_s \in \{0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 2.5, 1100\}$ using $\texttt{LensTools}$, and additionally provide halo catalogues and projected halo overdensity fields for galaxy--lensing cross-correlation analyses. We generate 100,000 realizations at the fiducial cosmology for the construction of the covariance matrix. This simulation suite provides a foundation for field-level inference and non-Gaussian statistics with next-generation weak lensing surveys such as the Rubin Observatory LSST, Euclid, and the Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope, as well as CMB lensing experiments including the Simons Observatory.

</details>


### [145] [Are Dynamical Dark-Energy Cosmologies Old Enough?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27209)
*Agripino Sousa-Neto,Susana J. Landau,Jailson Alcaniz*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过结合恒星年龄约束与DESI DR2、CMB和Pantheon数据，检验动态暗能量（w0-wa参数化）证据的稳健性；发现随着最小宇宙年龄下限提高（至13.85 Gyr），后验分布向ΛCDM模型（w0=-1, wa=0）显著偏移，其偏离显著性从2.56σ降至1.72σ，使ΛCDM进入95%可信区间，表明当前对动力学暗能量的统计偏好依赖于宇宙年龄先验，恒星年代学可作为关键稳健性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期基于BAO、CMB和Ia型超新星数据的分析显示时间依赖的暗能量状态方程（w0wa参数化）具有一定倾向性，但该结果是否稳健尚不明确；本文旨在利用独立的宇宙学一致性要求——即宇宙膨胀历史必须能容纳最古老恒星——来检验该结论的稳健性。

Method: 基于最新恒星与球状星团年龄测定结果，对DESI DR2+CMB+Pantheon联合数据施加逐步增强的宇宙年龄下限约束（t0 ≥ 13.6, 13.75, 13.8, 13.85 Gyr），在w0-wa参数空间中进行贝叶斯后验分析，并计算其相对于ΛCDM点（-1,0）的马氏距离变化。

Result: 随着最小允许宇宙年龄增加，w0-wa后验分布系统性地向ΛCDM预测值（-1,0）移动；当t0 ≥ 13.85 Gyr时，马氏距离由2.56σ减小至1.72σ，ΛCDM落入95%可信区域。

Conclusion: 当前对动力学暗能量的统计证据对宇宙年龄信息敏感；引入足够严格的恒星年龄下限可显著削弱对ΛCDM偏离的统计偏好，表明恒星年代学是检验DESI等巡天所支持膨胀历史稳健性的直接有效手段。

Abstract: Recent analysis combining baryon acoustic oscillation measurements with cosmic microwave background and Type Ia supernova data show an apparent preference for a time-dependent dark-energy equation of state within $w_0w_a$ parameterizations. Here we test the robustness of this result against an independent cosmological consistency requirement, namely that the expansion history must provide sufficient time to accommodate the oldest observed stellar populations. Motivated by recent stellar and globular-cluster age determinations, we impose progressively stronger lower bounds, $t_0 \geq 13.6$, $13.75$, $13.8$, and $13.85$ Gyr, on the expansion histories allowed by DESI DR2+CMB+Pantheon+ data. We find a systematic displacement of the posterior in the $(w_0,w_a)$ plane toward the $Λ$CDM prediction, $(w_0,w_a)=(-1,0)$, as the minimum allowed age is increased. For $t_0 \geq 13.85$ Gyr, the Mahalanobis separation from $Λ$CDM decreases from $2.56σ$ to $1.72σ$, bringing the $Λ$CDM prediction inside the 95\% credible region. Our results show that the current evidence for dynamical dark energy is sensitive to independent cosmic-age information and that stellar chronometry provides a direct robustness test of the DESI-favored expansion histories. Requiring sufficiently old cosmologies can appreciably reduce the statistical preference for departures from the standard $Λ$CDM model.

</details>


### [146] [Searching for Dense Perturbers in Cold Dark Matter Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27236)
*Xingyu Zhang,Hai-Bo Yu,Demao Kong,Ethan O. Nadler*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率的冷暗物质（CDM）模拟，系统检验了观测到的致密低质量天体（如引力透镜、恒星流扰动等）是否可由标准CDM子晕解释；结果表明，所有模拟子晕的内密度均低于观测推断值，凸显CDM模型与观测间的显著张力，并提示需探索罕见形成历史或新物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测发现若干低质量但高密度的天体扰动（来自引力透镜、恒星流、伴星系），若解释为暗物质子晕，则其内密度超出标准冷暗物质（CDM）模型预测，亟需定量检验该张力。

Method: 采用仅含CDM的银河系类比 zoom-in 数值模拟，分析超5000个质量低至10⁶ M⊙的子晕；利用潮汐截断模型重建其内密度轮廓，并在密度轮廓、内含质量、浓度参数及柱状投影质量等多个可观测量上与观测致密扰动进行系统比对；同时研究子晕浓度的红移演化并拟合其演化规律。

Result: 所有模拟的CDM子晕均不足以再现观测致密扰动所推断的质量分布——即其内密度普遍偏低；同时给出了子晕浓度随红移演化的拟合函数。

Conclusion: 标准CDM模型难以解释当前观测到的致密低质量扰动，该张力既可能源于极罕见的子晕形成历史，也可能暗示需要超越标准CDM的新物理机制。

Abstract: Recently, several low-mass, dense perturbers have been inferred from astrophysical observations of gravitational lensing, stellar streams, and satellite galaxies. If interpreted as dark matter subhalos, their inner densities exceed the predictions of the standard cold dark matter (CDM) model. We systematically investigate this tension using a CDM-only zoom-in simulation of a Milky Way analog. We analyze more than 5000 subhalos with masses down to $10^6\,M_\odot$ and reconstruct their inner density profiles using a tidal truncation model. The simulated subhalos are then compared with the observed compact perturbers in terms of their density profiles, enclosed masses, concentrations, and projected cylindrical masses. None of the simulated CDM subhalos is sufficiently dense to reproduce the inferred mass distributions of the observed perturbers. We further examine the redshift evolution of subhalo concentrations and derive a fitting function to characterize this evolution. Our findings motivate further investigations into exceptionally rare formation histories that could produce such dense perturbers, as well as the possibility of physics beyond the standard CDM model.

</details>


### [147] [The Future of Gamma-Ray Bursts as Probes of the Epoch of Reionization in the Era of JWST and the Extremely Large Telescope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27282)
*H. M. Fausey,A. J. van der Horst,N. R. Tanvir,K. Wiersema*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用高红移伽马暴（GRB）的Ly-alpha阻尼翼来测量再电离时期（EoR）星际介质中中性氢比例及宿主星系电离光子逃逸分数的方法，并通过模拟JWST NIRSpec和ELT HARMONI光谱仪对不同亮度和曝光时间的GRB观测能力，评估其在中性氢柱密度和中性分数测量上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 再电离时期（EoR）是宇宙学中尚未被充分理解的关键阶段；准确测量高红移GRB周围中性氢分数和宿主星系电离光子逃逸分数，对EoR建模至关重要。

Method: 基于JWST NIRSpec和ELT HARMONI光谱仪，对不同亮度与曝光时间的高红移GRB进行光谱模拟，分析其通过Ly-alpha阻尼翼测量中性氢柱密度和中性分数的能力，并考虑大气吸收线等实际观测限制。

Result: 两种光谱仪均能高效测定氢柱密度；ELT HARMONI在中性分数测量上表现强劲（尽管存在地气谱线干扰），而JWST NIRSpec对亮GRB及ELT低效红移区的中性分数测量不可或缺；缩短JWST ToO响应时间可显著提升中性分数测量精度。

Conclusion: JWST与ELT在互补波段和红移覆盖上协同工作，将极大推动EoR时期中性氢分布和星系光子逃逸物理的研究。

Abstract: The Epoch of Reionization (EoR) is a key era of cosmological history that remains poorly understood. An examination of the Ly-alpha damping wing of high-redshift GRBs can provide direct measurements of the fraction of neutral hydrogen (i.e., neutral fraction) of the IGM in the vicinity of the GRB host galaxy, in addition to the neutral column in the host along the sight-line to the GRB. The latter quantity can help infer the fraction of ionizing photons escaping the galaxy (i.e. escape fraction); the average escape fraction being a key input to EoR models. New and upcoming observatories such as the JWST and the Extremely Large Telescope (ELT) can obtain more robust estimates of the hydrogen column density and neutral fraction around high-redshift GRBs. We simulate spectra for the JWST NIRSpec and ELT HARMONI spectrographs using different GRB magnitudes and exposure times to determine their capabilities for measuring column densities and neutral fractions with the Ly-alpha damping wings of high-redshift GRBs. We find that both spectrographs will be highly effective for obtaining column density estimates. Despite the presence of telluric lines, we find that ELT HARMONI will be a powerful instrument for retrieving accurate neutral fraction estimates, but JWST NIRSpec will be vital for obtaining neutral fraction measurements for bright GRBs that fall in redshift ranges where ELT is less effective. Additionally, shorter JWST Target of Opportunity (ToO) follow-up times would greatly increase the accuracy of neutral fraction estimates obtained with NIRSpec spectra.

</details>


### [148] [Probing Minihalos in the Late Dark Ages through Velocity Acoustic Oscillations in the 21-cm Power Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27933)
*Yan Dong,Bin Yue,Chen Zhang,Yuewei Wen,Yidong Xu,Ayodeji Ibitoye,Xuelei Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在宇宙黑暗时代，由于暗物质-重子相对流速抑制小质量晕气体吸积，从而在21 cm功率谱中产生速度声学振荡（VAO）特征，为探测原初小质量晕提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黑暗时代，小质量晕与IGM的21 cm信号混合，难以分离其贡献；而晚期黑暗时代IGM信号减弱、且相对流速调制小晕分布，提供了独特探测窗口。

Method: 理论建模分析小质量晕气体吸积受相对流速影响的机制，计算其在21 cm功率谱中产生的VAO特征，并评估不同望远镜配置下的可探测性。

Result: 在z∼15和z∼20处发现显著VAO首峰信号（分别为~1.4×10⁻³和~3.1×10⁻⁴ mK²），通过大型射电阵列（数万至数十万偶极子）经数十天积分可探测；若小尺度物质功率谱增强，所需阵列规模可进一步缩小。

Conclusion: VAO特征是探测黑暗时代小质量晕的有力探针，尤其适用于未来低频射电阵列（如Lunar-based或SKA-low）开展高精度21 cm功率谱观测。

Abstract: In the Dark Ages, minihalos above the Jeans mass accrete gas from the intergalactic medium (IGM). The accreted gas would be heated to virial temperature and produces 21 cm signal in emission, while the majority of gas retained in the IGM is cold and has 21 cm signal in absorption. The 21 cm signal of the IGM generally dominates over that of minihalos, making it difficult to extract the contribution of minihalos from the detected 21 cm signal. However, in the late Dark Ages, in regions where the gas has not yet been influenced by the radiation from Pop III stars, the 21 cm signal from the IGM is weak because collisional coupling has become less efficient. More importantly, the relic supersonic dark matter-baryon relative streaming velocities suppress the gas accretion of smaller minihalos and encode their large-scale coherent features into the 21 cm signal, generating the velocity acoustic oscillation (VAO) features on the 21 cm power spectrum. This offers a promising avenue for detecting minihalos. We found that, at $z\sim 15$ ($\sim 20$), the first peak of the net VAO wiggle at $k=0.05$ Mpc$^{-1}$ is $\sim 1.4\times 10^{-3}$ ($\sim 3.1\times 10^{-4}$) mK$^2$. With a total of 24 lunar nights/8064 hr (60 lunar nights/20160 hr) of integration, the signal from $z\sim 15$ ($\sim 20$) is detectable for an array composed of 82944 (278784) dipoles, spread over $\sim 3.5$ ($\sim 6.3$) km. If the small-scale matter power spectrum is enhanced and minihalos are much more numerous than in the standard $Λ$CDM model, the signal is detectable for an even smaller scale array.

</details>


### [149] [Slow-Roll Inflation at Arbitrary Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28608)
*William Giarè*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文推导了慢滚近似下任意有限阶p的原初标量和张量谱的显式表达式，适用于标准单场暴胀和k-暴胀，并给出了连接张量倾角、其跑动及标量-张量比、标量谱指数及其跑动的闭合形式一致性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一处理不同慢滚阶数下的暴胀原初谱与一致性关系，避免依赖扰动修正的模函数，提升理论普适性与计算效率。

Method: 基于自由dS解确定普适系数，构建慢滚参数的加权组合；发展生成算法，直接导出任意阶(p,n)下的谱与一致性关系。

Result: 获得任意有限阶p的标量/张量谱显式公式；推导出适用于任意n≥0和p≥0的一致性关系，涵盖至N10LO；结果兼容k-暴胀（含声速及其尺度导数）。

Conclusion: 慢滚展开中谱与一致性关系具有高度结构化形式，其系数由对称性（dS不变性）完全决定，无需递归或数值拟合，为高阶暴胀检验提供解析基础。

Abstract: We derive explicit expressions for the primordial scalar and tensor spectra at arbitrary finite order $p$ in the slow-roll expansion, valid both for canonical single-field inflation and $k$-inflation. At any order, the spectra are determined by known combinations of slow-roll parameters weighted by universal coefficients entirely fixed by the free de Sitter solutions, without requiring perturbatively corrected mode functions. Using these spectra, we obtain closed-form consistency relations that connect the tensor tilt $n_t$ and its runnings $α_n^t\equiv d^n n_t/d(\ln k)^n$ to the tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$, the scalar spectral index $n_s$, and its runnings $α_n^s\equiv d^n(n_s-1)/d(\ln k)^n$. The consistency relations are valid for arbitrary $n\geq 0$ and any finite $p\geq 0$, without recursion over lower-order relations. For $k$-inflation, they additionally depend on the scalar sound speed and its scale derivatives. We develop an algorithm that generates both the spectra and consistency relations for any pair $(n,p)$. We reproduce known results through next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order (N3LO) and extend the explicit calculations up to N10LO.

</details>
